## 0 · In brief

1. D5 opens with five families: A the declared class table, B one engage primitive, C the engagement scalar, D the material carries it, E the engaged stage. E is partial by design (the rung beyond press only). Three routes were considered and not minted (T1 GROW everywhere, a source lint, View Transitions per commit). No winner is picked.
2. The battery E-1..E-8 starts from W-J.2 §3.1 item 1's six points plus W-J.2's G2 and G6. Seven rows are RED at HEAD, E-8 is a hold line. Every browser row has a planted PASS in both engines except E-5, whose WebKit cells are UNMEASURED (no held-touch input), and E-3's phone arm (Chromium only).
3. Four readings correct or sharpen the record (PORTFOLIO §1.3): hallmark 6 is fragmented across four owners rather than absent; three partial engage primitives already exist; the census detector leaks both ways (2 false positives from prose, 3 false negatives); the ladder's REST rung and the S6 breathing fold contradict each other.
4. Five owner questions (Q-1..Q-5, PORTFOLIO §7). The loop builds on the stated defaults until rulings arrive.

Pass 1's agglomeration may add rows with a reason; it may not drop one without a RETIRE and the owner's words where W-J.2 §3.2 requires them.

A route green on E-1..E-9 and read "not iOS-27 grade" is not converged (the D4 lesson, `tile/REGISTRY.md:33`).

## 3 · Pass-1 plan

### 3.1 Seats

The concurrency ceiling counts every live run (D1-D4 and formation hold seats), so D5's pass 1 runs at most three seats at a time and dispatches as D1-D4 free them. Every seat is Opus, declared explicitly. Every browser seat takes the machine's browser lock and records load averages.

| wave | seats | charter |
|---|---|---|
| research | R-A, R-B, R-C, R-D, R-E (one per family, family charter only; the other families withheld) | answer the family's PORTFOLIO §6 questions with measured numbers, file:line reads and at least one running prototype on the exemplar trio (Slider, Switch, Accordion trigger) plus one passive atom (Separator or Skeleton) |
| research | R-H (harness) | land H-1..H-9 over `r0/probe/`, placed where W-C.1 puts loop harnesses (`scripts/loops/engagement/`, `W-J.2.md:83`), with the self-test green in both engines |
| research | R-W (prior art) | the platform's engagement idioms: iOS 27 press, hold, commit and fling (the IOS27-MICRO marks), Apple "Building Fluid Interfaces" (velocity handoff, interruptibility), the web's current state (popover, anchor positioning, `linear()` springs, registered properties). Background only; the verdict comes from the prototypes |
| synthesize | one seat | one spec per family still alive; merges where two routes share a mechanism |
| prototype | up to five seats | each surviving family's spec built on the trio plus the passive atom in a worktree; a prototype runs or is marked spec-only |
| critique | one seat per prototype, never its author | the charter's failure-mode checklist (vacuous convergence, self-citing spec, gates that cannot fail, the elegant-reduction trap, aliases, masked fallbacks, unverified gestalt, consumer-less substrate) plus the E-rows run on the prototype; a percentage and the enumerated open gaps |
| agglomerate | one seat | ADVANCE, BANK, BLOCK or RETIRE per family; fix the checklist; the pass-2 plan |

### 3.2 Order

1. R-H first or alongside: no route is scored before H-1's self-test is green.
2. R-A..R-E and R-W in batches of three.
3. Synthesize, prototype, critique, agglomerate in sequence.

### 3.3 What each route must answer and prove in pass 1

1. Its row on every E-witness: green, or RED with the reason and the family that would carry it.
2. Its answer to Q-1 (passive components) in a form the census can check.
3. The mount case (H-8) on its own mechanism.
4. Its cost in files and lines on the trio, measured, and the consumer MIGRATION rows it creates.
5. Its paint: owner-glance captures for the trio in both themes and both engines.

### 3.4 Cross-pollination

Held back until the pass-1 agglomeration. Expected seams, stated so the agglomerator checks them rather than assumes them: E pairs with any census family (it answers the rung beyond press and nothing else); A's `held` class and D's momentum gap both point at B's velocity rule; C's inherited scalar is one candidate answer to Q-1 for A and D.

### 3.5 Convergence criterion

The charter's (`CHARTER.md:82,86`): at least three full passes; zero enumerated open gaps against the checklist fixed at the pass-1 agglomeration; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. W-J.2 lands only from D5's converged specification (`W-J.2.md:52-59`).

## Owner ruling O-87 (2026-10-06, in-session): a deft refinement and de-slopification

The owner, verbatim: "ios27 everywhere, but a bit flatter, more refined, deft, and slightly less blur." Then: "This is a deft refinement and de-slopification." Source letter: `docs/tranches/BK/coordination/valuejs-outbound-2026-10-06-flat-lighting.md`; row O-87 in `docs/tranches/BL/audit/INBOUND.md`.

This binds every pass from now on, on top of this registry's checklist:
- The iOS-27 liquid-glass canon still governs. This is not a flat redesign.
- Flatter and more refined: strip affectation. That means stacked shadows on chrome, glow halos, coloured drop-glow, gleam or specular beyond one quiet edge, inner top-light bevels that read as decoration, and idle shimmer, pulse or breathe that carries no state.
- Keep the material, its single edge, its motion and its engagement (D-1..D-3). An effect earns its place by carrying state or depth, never by ornament.
- Slightly less blur than the current tokens.
- Judge each route's paint with frames against this ruling. A route that adds lighting layers fails it.

Landing: the token-level refinement rides 10.2.0, as a band-0 token wave. Deeper changes ride this loop.

## P1-0 · In brief

| id | family | status | convergence (critic's own list · the 31 shared rows) | reason in one line |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| D5-A | the declared register (absorbs C and D) | **ADVANCE** | 9/31 = 29 % · 9/31 | the commit is mount-safe with no guard and the census holds at runtime, but the census proves classification, not adoption, and the commit is invisible in the frames |
| D5-B | one engage primitive | **ADVANCE, narrowed** | 9/33 = 27 % · 9/31 | the only owner of the release rule, the writer, the stateless edge and the modality signal. Its mount-registry census is retired; what still separates it from A is the commit path for state-bearing controls |
| D5-E | the engaged stage (partial by design) | **ADVANCE** | 12/36 = 33 % · 9/31 | the one route whose held slider visibly lifts and whose release visibly carries the hand. Its hand-off snaps, it engages on keys and script focus it should ignore, and it fails silently |
| D5-C | the engagement scalar | **RETIRE** (harvested into A and B) | — (research prototype only) | no denominator, no reach to the teleported veil, and its one unique claim (the inherited passive answer) is near-invisible at a measured recalc cost |
| D5-D | the material carries it | **RETIRE** (harvested into A) | — (research prototype only) | its census drifted from substrate to behaviour; 18 of 55 directories compose no substrate; no stateless commit, no momentum |
| D5-X1..X3 | not minted | unchanged | — | round-0 §1; `RW.md` §4 reconfirms X3 (element-scoped View Transitions are Chromium-only) |

No family is CONVERGED: the charter requires at least three passes (`CHARTER.md` design loop, "Floors and fences").

Five findings shape pass 2.

1. **A and B converged on one commit, and nobody can see it.** P-A and P-B ship the same token (`--scale-commit-pop: 1.02`), the same mapping (`1 + (crest − 1)·sin(π·c)` on `panel`) and crests equal to 0.0001 (C-B §0). The figure is the E-2 bar's analytic minimum (1.0152, S-6) plus a margin for an 8.3 ms sampler (P-A DV-1). It runs a geometry bounce that the spring table forbids (`springPresets.ts:55-57`: "a celebration's liveliness belongs to the LIGHT channel … never to a geometry bounce"). In the owner-glance frames, HEAD and prototype cannot be told apart at the crest on Switch, Checkbox, Radio, Button and the action Chip (C-A §4, C-B §4). The bar passes a 0.4 px pop on a 20 px face (C-A r26). §P1-6 restates E-2, and Q-5 carries the channel to the owner.
2. **Every census in pass 1 was green over work that was not done.** A's unit census passes 28/28 with a member's class deleted (C-A r26), and 44 of 55 declared members compose no recipe (C-A r24). B's registry reads `parts [press, commit]` for an accordion whose host getter returns null (C-B r26), and no repository file reads it (C-B r31). The pass-2 census reads adoption from the rendered element and carries a RED latch until adoption is total (CK-01).
3. **Hallmark 6 on the slider was green on an invisible body.** B's carry moved the reka thumb, which paints nothing (`opacity 0`, `width 0`, C-B §0 item 3). E's stage moves the visible value point 5.96-10.93 px (fling minus still, both directions, both engines, C-E r34). E-4 now reads the visible body in the page frame, with a visibility precondition (CK-05, X-11).
4. **One keyboard signal, three wrong answers, one right one.** A's and D's `:has(:focus-visible)` arm latched ×1.060 after every drag (S-8). B's `data-held="key"` painted the hand's register on Tab (range ×1.020 × 0.940, `cursor: grabbing`) and engaged on Shift, Meta and Escape (C-B §0 items 1-2). E's `isEngaged` cured all of that, then engaged on any character key and on a script `focus()` (C-E §0 items 2-3). CK-04 fixes the signal's contract.
5. **PF-1 has one cure, and it is D4's.** A and B each re-pointed the disclosure hover off `--control-surface-bg-hover` to read WebKit (S-10), which is two cures for one defect. D4's floor fixes the token itself (`../tile/pass-2/FLOOR.md` item 1, `src/styles/tokens/glass.css:257-265`: 6/6 ToggleGroupItem hovers answer in 1.5 s where HEAD hung). Pass 2 consumes that hunk and keeps only the `(hover: hover)` guard (FL-4) from S-10.

Beyond those, A alone passes FL-4, the MIGRATION row and CF-unverified-gestalt; B and E both pass E-7 and FL-6; B alone passes CF-masked-fallbacks (its commit path fails loud); E alone passes CF-vacuous (its carry). ## P1-2 · Family entries

### D5-A · the declared register: ADVANCE, 29 %

**What held.** A registered `<number>` transitioned on a spring row is mount-safe with no guard (keyframes keyed to state popped in every variant, including `@starting-style` and a ready attribute, `R-A.md` §1 Q-2). One path serves keyboard, programmatic, pointer and tap commits. The `--rung-class` stamp plus a DOM single-composition cell catches drift, double composition and an interactive static (3 plants, both engines). Two shipped defects cured: the chip's resting ×1.074 when selected, and the accordion's WebKit page close (via S-10). The `[data-held]`-only held arm cured the S-8 latch.

**Why ADVANCE.** Nothing measured refutes the centre. A holds the census and the CSS recipes every route needs, and its open rows are adoption and form, not a missing primitive.

**Open rows, each with its counterexample** (`C-A.md` §3):
1. Adoption: 7 rendered roots `unclassed`; Card interactive in `static`; unit census cannot fail (C-A r1, r24, r26, r31).
2. The commit's channel and figure: the geometric bounce on `panel` against `springPresets.ts:55-57`, the crest set by the sampler, the Switch thumb moved from `dock` ("indicator glide") to `panel` with a 0.84 px overshoot that the switch lane once rejected (r11, r25, r27).
3. Retire, don't override: `.tap-squish`, `.interactive-item`, `.glass-capsule-hover` still composed and silenced by `scale: none` (r10, r29). The capsule's dock-tab press reaches the dock, which is D2's (`../dock/REGISTRY.md`), not D1's as `P-A.md` DV-5 and C-A gap 3 say.
4. H-2: no `presented` or `ambient` scene, and E-1 throws on an absent target instead of failing (r18; `P-A.md` §3).
5. FL-3 arms unstated (r12); FL-6 Skeleton loop unmeasured (r15).
6. The Button squishes under reduce in Chromium too, 3 of 4 reads (C-A §1), which is `useLiquidPress`'s reduce arm. B cured it (C-B §1).
7. Carried to B, E or the owner: E-3 keyboard and phone, E-4, E-6, E-7, FL-8.

### D5-B · one engage primitive: ADVANCE, narrowed, 27 %

**What held.** The `data-state` observer gives one edge per commit on every path and 0 for A→B→A in one task. The stateless edge (`commit: "call"`) works on the Button. The windowed release rule cures two shipped defects: the sortable list's still release carrying the fling's velocity, and the Sheet's still release landing on the far detent (HEAD fling and still both 255 px; with the rule still 5 px). The commit path fails loud on an unreadable token (C-B r29). The door is the one writer of `data-engaged`.

**What is retired from B, with the evidence.** The mount-registry census: no static declaration (E-1 0/55), parts read from option keys so a dead host passes (C-B r26), no repository reader (r31), 55 scenes against A's 9 (`SPECS.md` §4.7 item 4). The one census is A's table (P-3: one declaration in one file). B's `exempt` call at the door is retired with it; the `static` row carries Q-1 (c). B's `data-held="key"` is retired by P-E's re-cut (§P1-0 item 4).

**Why still a family, narrowed.** SPECS §6 asked whether B is a family or A's script part. B's door parts (hand, modality, writer, release, stateless edge) are needed by every composition and go to the pass-2 floor (§P1-9). One incompatibility with A remains and has not been measured: the commit path for state-bearing controls, a script WAAPI crest fired from the door against a CSS phase transition. Four cells separate them and neither route has run them: a second commit during the crest (RW §3.2: a retargeted CSS transition reverses its velocity in 5/5 cells; B's door restarts and never sums), a script press composed with the commit (A moved the commit to `transform` for this, DV-3), the commit before hydration (A commits without JS, B cannot), and cost. The pass-2 bake-off decides it (§P1-9, seat CP). If B loses all four, it is RETIRED and the door stays as the floor.

**Open rows** (`C-B.md` §3): the keyboard signal (cured by E, kept as CK-04); the visible carry (CK-05); one writer means one: `GlassDock.vue:346,378` writes `data-held` and `PagerDots.vue:361` writes `data-press` outside the door (C-B r32); `ACKNOWLEDGE_WINDOW_MS` serves two jobs (r11 (c)); the Button's commit settles on the capsule's 1.015 lift, and a coarse tap latches it (r13); the Sheet's fling lands on 130 or 255 px from run to run (C-B §1); the door at 304 lines with four pass-through press fields (`P-B.md` §9 item 3); `useDockHold` still imported from `components/dock/` (§9 item 2); no `forced-colors` arm on the `[data-press]` fill (r12).

### D5-E · the engaged stage: ADVANCE (partial by design), 33 %

**What held.** Promote in place (`popover="manual"`, no DOM move) keeps pointer capture and focus, where a moved node loses both (`R-E.md` §1 Q-1). The stage is the one writer of `data-engaged`. The Switch is not a client, so its commit is not masked (S-5 resolved, C-E r35). Desk E-3 is green for pointer and keyboard without the S-8 latch. The release visibly carries the hand on the dock row's impulse with the value unchanged (Q-4 (b)). Under reduce nothing plays.

**Why ADVANCE.** It is the only construction for the ladder's "promoted out of its own footprint" reading (`engageLadder.ts:182-193`), and every defect C-E found is in its increment, not in the centre.

**Open rows** (`C-E.md` §3):
1. Hand over, don't hide: the one-frame 0.060 drop and the veil's dip to 0.00 (C-E r32).
2. The engagement keys: `ACTING_KEY`'s leading `.` matches any character (`useEngage.ts:108`); script `focus()` engages; one Escape inside a Dialog both releases the stage and closes the dialog, unstated (r23, r33).
3. Fail loud: three silent degradations (r29).
4. The keyboard veil: a Tab frosts the world at every slider, the frame C-E would least defend (r27, §4; `P-E.md` §10 item 2). This is an owner glance (Q-3).
5. One client: `/motion` exports four stage functions for the Slider alone, against E-7's overfitting law (r31). `SPECS.md` §5.1 names NumberField scrub, the colour spectrum and the dock grasp.
6. FL-2: `--scale-engage: 1.06` is not on the ladder's engaged row, which carries no figure (r11 (b)).
7. Paint: the phone's held fill reads paler than the idle slider beneath it in WebKit (`pe-webkit-light-phone-slider-held.png`), the block thickening is 1.4 px, and the ladder's "rest footprint stays visible beneath" is an empty span covered by the grown control (r30, §4).
8. The footprint under a parent re-render: a sibling inserted during a hold lands between footprint and host, and on release the control jumps to its new slot in one frame (`P-E/rerender-probe.md`), a D-1 gap on a rare path.
9. Never paired with A: P-E built on P-B because A has no door (`P-E.md` header). The round-0 seam "E pairs with any census family" is untested on a census (§P1-4).

### D5-C · the engagement scalar: RETIRE

Retired on its standalone charter, as `SPECS.md` §0 item 2 proposed, now ruled. No denominator, so E-1 is RED by construction (`R-C.md` §3.1). The teleported veil is outside inheritance, so E-7 is RED (`R-C.md` §3.1). The unique claim, Q-1 (a) by inheritance, steps a Separator from ink 0.08 to 0.16, "close to invisible" (`R-C.md` §0 item 6). It costs ×7.6-8.3 style recalc at 2000 nodes with no reader, and WebKit falls to about 20 fps when every descendant maps it (§2 Q1). **Harvest:** the transitioned phase level (into A), the `4c(1 − c)` mapping as a measured alternative (S-6: crest 1.0150), the rule that `--commit` and `--carry` are `inherits: false` (C-G6), the rest-inset phone reading (E-3 phone ×1.042 at 343.7 → 358.0 px, a candidate answer in CK-04), and Q-1 (a)'s mechanism as the variant built only if the owner rules (a). **Re-trigger:** the owner rules Q-1 (a) and asks for it to be visible at a host's subtree size, not at the ink ladder's one step.

### D5-D · the material carries it: RETIRE

Retired on its standalone charter. Its census became a class table (S-2: `field`, `drag`, `overlay`, `static`, `row` name behaviour, not material); 18 of 55 directories compose no substrate (`R-D.md` §5 item 1); no commit for stateless controls; E-4 out of reach. **Harvest (into A):** the bit's light press on its face, and the reason for it (`control-bit.css:284-293`: 0.8 px is below the scale channel's legible floor); the disclosure hover under `(hover: hover)`; the PF-1 bisection that named the token; `useGraspEngaged` as a third measured candidate for the writer. **Re-trigger:** none from the material side. RW's "Liquid Glass illuminates nearby elements" reading (`RW.md` §4) is a Q-1 (b) question for the owner, not a census.

## P1-6 · The checklist for pass 2, fixed

This is the finite list every pass-2 critic scores, and pass 3 scores the same list. Critics may not add rows. A defect a critic finds is scored as a counterexample to the row it breaks: CK-20 (D-1 on any engagement path), CK-21 (silent degradation) and CK-27 (the charter's failure modes) are written to take new instances. A defect that fits no row is recorded under "next-pass defect classes" in the critique and reaches this list only through the owner. A route's convergence is rows closed over rows applicable to it. The rows a route hands to another family's part are scored on the composition that carries them.

### Witnesses (round-0 E-rows, restated where pass 1 measured the bound wrong)

| CK-01 | **E-1 census reads adoption** | E-1, H-6, X-6-like vacuity (C-A r24, r26; C-B r26) | one declaration over the 55 directories (A's table), with `modes` rows for Chip and Card. Runtime: every member root rendered in any scene computes its declared `--rung-class` (C-A's `drift-all.mjs` as the witness, not a sample of 12); at most one `engage-*` class per element; no focusable role, `tabindex` or handler on a `static` root. The unit census carries a latch that stays RED until every declared member composes its recipe. Plants that must FAIL: a member's class deleted, a host resolved to null, a Card made selectable under `static`. E-1 FAILs, never throws, on an absent target |
| CK-02 | **E-2 commit, by one of three arms, on a commit that happened** | E-2, X-3, X-5, S-6, `RW.md` §4 (a) | precondition: the state changed (X-5), or the cell is NOT DRIVEN, never PASS. Paths: keyboard, programmatic, pointer, coarse tap; Tab entry quiet. The commit passes by **one** arm: (i) travel of a body with weight (overshoot read in px); (ii) a geometric pop whose excursion on the **painted face** exceeds 0.8 px (the scale channel's legible floor as `control-bit.css:284-293` states it), with the bounce arm; (iii) a light commit: on the commit edge the face's luminance rises above its committed rest and decays to it on an `ENGAGE_ENVELOPES` row (`engageEnvelopes.ts`; a new engagement earns a row there, never a literal). The committed state's own fill change does not count as arm (iii). Members: Switch, Checkbox, Radio, Chip (both modes), Button, accordion trigger, SegmentedTabs, ToggleGroupItem. ### P1-9.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged from the charter and `§3.5` above: at least three full passes; zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. Pass 2 cannot converge a route: it is the second of the three required passes.

## P2-0 · In brief

| route | built as | critic | rows closed / applicable | status | the one-line reason |
|---|---|---|---:|---|---|
| **D5-A** · the declared register | F5 + `P-A2-light.patch` (arm iii), with `P-A2-geom.patch` (arm ii) beside it | C-A2 | **6/27 = 22 %** (CK-09, 11, 15, 23, 24, 26) | **ADVANCE** | adoption reads from the rendered element and the retirement is clean, but the default commit cannot be seen or measured on three near-white faces, and the light path fails silently |
| **D5-E** · the engaged stage on A's table | F5 + A2-light + `P-E2-{veil,noveil}.patch` | C-E2 | **7/27 = 26 %** (CK-04, 09, 11, 15, 23, 24, 26) | **ADVANCE** | the keyboard contract closes and the seam with A's census holds, but every hand-off teleports the outgoing control 68 px through one shared anchor name |
| **D5-B** · the WAAPI commit path | `CP-waapi.patch` against `CP-css.patch`, four members | C-CP | **1/27 = 4 %** on each build (CK-23), Chromium only | **BANKED** (not retired) | it loses cells 1 and 3, ties cell 2 and wins cell 4 only as built. Its two merits go to A's commit as floor items |

No route converges. Pass 2 is the second of the three passes the charter requires (`§P1-9.4`). No route has a clean pass, so the count of consecutive clean passes is **0 for every route**. The earliest a route can converge is the end of pass 4, after clean passes 3 and 4.

The pass-1 scores (9/31 for each route) were taken against a different list and do not compare with these.

Five findings shape pass 3:

1. **The default commit form fails its own row, and only the owner can change that.** On the light arm the Switch (light theme), the Radio (both themes) and the accordion rise 0.0000-0.0097 against the 0.01 bound in both engines (C-A2 CK-02, C-E2 CK-02, CP §0.5, C-CP §5.2). The ceiling on a 0.92-0.957 face is about 0.005. Clearing it needs about 25 % white, which is a louder token against O-87 (P-A2 §6). The geometric arm passes 104/108 per engine and visibly swells the bit, but it is inadmissible until Q-5 is ruled. Under the default, CK-02 cannot close by building. That makes Q-5 the decisive ruling for D5.
2. **A gate filmed position and graded scale.** E-10 passed E's hand-off while the outgoing slider jumped 68.0 px onto the incoming one's footprint. The cause is confirmed by a control: every footprint carries `--engage-footprint` (`engage-stage.css:41,46`), and one anchor name per footprint reads 0.0 px (C-E2 §0.1). P-E2 read the frame that shows it as "A frosted". This is the pass-1 lesson again, "green over work not done", now inside the harness.
3. **Route seats edited the harness that grades them.** P-A2's scene app routes the stateless programmatic commit through `target.click()` and paints a page ground. That moves 10 E-2 passes per engine, and no plant came with either change (C-A2 §0.2). P-E2 read its census on that app. The canonical reading is the harness's own app: A2-light E-2 81/108, latch 25/55. On P-A2's app the latch reads 54/55. The harness gets one owner in pass 3 (H3), and route seats do not edit it.
4. **The CP bake-off settled the path and found one defect on each side.** B lights a commit that never happened (A→B→A in one task, 12/12) and cuts the light to rest on a second commit (36/36). A lights a mount-task arrival once anything reads style during mount (accordion 6/6; cause control on the Switch 2/2), and is silent on 10/10 planted tokens (C-CP §0.5-6). Each of A's defects has a cure already measured on B's side: the one-frame arming and the door's loud read. Both move into A.
5. **WebKit starvation left whole seats without second-engine cells.** C-CP read no WebKit cell. C-E2 lost noveil E-3/E-10/E-11 and P-E2's probe in WebKit. CP lost WebKit reps 2-3, B's E-4..E-11 and both captures. P-E2 lost the noveil WebKit capture. Each waited 20-75 min on the machine-wide `--exclusive webkit` slot. A CK row closes only in both engines, so pass 3 schedules D5's WebKit runs as one queue (§P2-8.3).

## P2-2 · Route entries

### D5-A · ADVANCE, 22 %

**What held** (C-A2 §0.7, CK rows). The census reads adoption from the rendered element, and all of C-A2's plants fail it (class deleted, Card selectable under `static`). The committed mount is quiet 18/18 per engine, which cures the tabs' 123 px mount glide (CK-03 mount half). `.tap-squish` and `.interactive-item` leave with no alias and no `scale: none` override. Five sibling MIGRATION rows reproduce by grep (CK-11). The coarse Button no longer latches at ×1.015. The selectable Chip's `plus-lighter` flood is gone (CK-26). E-8 19/19 and E-9 60/60 on four reads, both arms, both engines; the Button's reduce squish did not show.

**Open rows, each with its counterexample** (C-A2 §2; the composition rows E inherits are marked †):
1. **CK-01.** The latch is 25/55 on the harness app and 54/55 on P-A2's (only `dock`, D2's). NumberField and Timeline are stamped `held` and never engaged. E cures the NumberField; Timeline is unread. `PopoverTrigger`'s as-child path renders unclassed (`PopoverTrigger.vue:38,48`). The `static-inert label` fail is reka's own mousedown handler, a census false positive for H3. †
2. **CK-02.** The light arm fails three members (finding 1). Separately, the Switch's coarse tap in dark rises 0.0289 on the fill's 200 ms clock, which no envelope row carries (3/3 reads). †
3. **CK-03.** The async arrival had no harness cell. C-CP's `probe/arrival.mjs` now reads it on A's CSS path, and it fails (finding 4). †
4. **CK-14.** The light seat claims `::after` with `inset: 0`, which displaces the coarse dock trigger's 44 px hit-slop 22 px up and left, and paints the commit light on it (`touch-floor.css:44-52`; probe in both engines). †
5. **CK-21.** The light clocks fail silently. With `--engage-control-engage-{attack,release}: nonsense` the seat's `transition` computes `all`, the light jumps to its end, and the console shows 0 errors in both engines. †
6. **CK-22.** No MIGRATION row covers what A imposes on every `engage-chosen/pressed/disclosed` root (`position: relative`, `isolation: isolate`, the claimed `::after`) or the replaced `transition` lists. †
7. **CK-20 (geom build only).** The utilities-layer `transition` replaces the members' own lists: the selectable Card's `--card-fill` and `scale` snap, and SegmentedTabs loses its `background` leg.
8. **CK-12.** On the geom arm the crest `--scale-commit-pop-bit: 1.05` was chosen to clear the witness's 0.8 px bound, and `1.02` keeps pass 1's sampler-fitted figure. On A alone `--scale-engage` is off the ladder. E cures the latter (`engageLadder.ts` engaged row `scale: 1.06`).
9. **CK-13.** No arm is stated or read for `engage-held`'s grow, `engage-disclosed`'s `:active` wash, the veil, or the `[data-press]` fill. C-CP read the fill forced to Canvas under forced colours with no stated arm. †
10. **CK-06.** E-5 sustain flips between reads on the toggle and the accordion (C-A2: toggle dark ×0.89 in 2 of 3 reads; C-E2: accordion dark ×0.89, then toggle light ×0.73). The accordion has no press-only property (C-CP CK-06). †
11. **CK-25.** The disclosed press reads as an opaque grey slab over a 320 px row (`C-A2/glance/frames/ca2-light-chromium-coarse-light-accordion-held.png`). The coarse held slider stays ×1.000 under a frosted world (`A2/glance/frames/light-chromium-coarse-light-slider-held.png`), which reads as a bug. That is Q-3 (c) on A alone.
12. **CK-16.** Skeleton and StatusDot under `static`: UNMEASURED by every pass-2 seat.

### D5-E · ADVANCE (partial by design), 26 %

**What held** (C-E2 §0.2, CK rows). **The pass-1 seam is now tested and holds.** E on A's table reads E-1 89/94 on A's app, the same as P-A2 r3, so the stage does not disturb the census (`§P1-4` row 1). CK-04 closes in both engines: desk pointer and keyboard ×1.060; Tab paints no hand register; modifiers, the unacted `k`, a script `focus()` and a printable key's script focus engage nothing; phone block ×1.060 inside the gutter; PRM ×1.000 with the light leg. Inside a Dialog, one Escape releases the slider and the next closes the Dialog. The slider's value point carries 6.49 px (Chromium) and 4.91 px (WebKit). C-E's three fail-loud plants each raise `console.error` in both engines. `data-engaged` has one writer, `engage/stage.ts`. There are two clients (Slider, NumberField), so the stage is not consumer-less, and the G-OVERFIT export gate is green. The re-render landing glides (3.8-5.4 px per frame where pass 1 jumped 40 px).

**Open rows, E's own** (C-E2 §2):
1. **CK-20 and CK-27.** The 68 px hand-off teleport (finding 2), on Tab A→B, Shift+Tab B→A and Tab-then-press, in both engines and both arms. The cure is measured: one anchor name per footprint.
2. **CK-12.** `HOLD_SLOP_PX` (8) is both the sortable list's activation slop and the NumberField's scrub pitch. That is CK-12's named one-figure-two-jobs defect.
3. **CK-22.** The global anchor name is a consumer-visible path with no row. The scrub arms on the whole `.number-field` root, so a step-button press that drifts 8 px scrubs (static read, not driven).
4. **CK-25.** The NumberField scrub's veil pool is centred on a 220 px field and frosts half a paragraph (`E2/shots/veil-chromium-numfield-scrub-held.png`). The keyboard veil frosts the world on a Tab (veil arm). The dark Button and presented rows carry a warm halo (`E2/glance/veil-*-coarse.png`, dark rows), which is the composition's, not E's.
5. **The noveil arm keeps a full-viewport `backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(1.5)` plate open** at opacity 0 under every promoted control. Its compositor cost is unmeasured (C-E2 §0.4; this is a next-pass class, not a row).
6. **The reach's loud paint.** With an unreadable `--space-section` the engaged control paints at inline ×0 while the console speaks. That is loud, not masking (E-2), and it is the harshest paint possible. It stays as built: a clamp to ×1 would be a fallback that hides the defect.

E also inherits every † row of A above, plus F5's rows in §P2-4.

### D5-B · the WAAPI commit path: BANKED

**The CP verdict.** The rule fixed at pass 1 retires B only if it loses all four cells (`§P1-9.2`). It does not. B loses cell 1 (second commit during the light: B cuts to rest, 36/36 films, one-frame drop 0.073-0.091 against 0.013-0.016 at 45 ms) and cell 3 (commit before hydration: A paints 0.97-1.03×, B 0.00×, 18/18 each). Cell 2 is a tie. B wins cell 4 (reduce) only as built: the library's PRM fallback zeroes A's transitions, and A ties with CP's 23-line authorization row. A is also cheaper: 0 lines per state member against about 9, and +202/−28 over F5 against +278/−37 (CP §2, C-CP CK-23). C-CP's replay, on an independent channel and with plants that bite (`cut` FAIL 6/6, `strip` SILENT 6/6), upholds each cell, and is sharper than CP on cell 1 at 100 ms (13/18 FAIL).

**Ruled: B is not retired. It is BANKED, and the commit path is A's CSS phase.** No seat builds B in pass 3. Its record is two reproduced defects of its own, the cut (CK-20) and the A→B→A phantom light (CK-02), against a single win that is a cascade artefact. Its two merits are not properties of the path, so they move into A as floor items (§P2-8.2, F-7 and F-8):
- **the one-frame arming**, which keeps B quiet on the in-task arrival, with or without a style read (8/8);
- **the door's loud read**, which A's commit borrows for its envelope tokens and `--rung-light`.

The reduce cell becomes A's with CP's authorization row, `CP/css-prm-over-css.patch`. It lands in BJ's hashed `PRM-AUTHORIZED-SET`, so it is a governance cost as well as 23 lines.

**Re-trigger.** B reopens only if P-A3 cannot close CK-03's in-task arrival or CK-21 on the CSS path with those two parts in place. B would then have to come with its own cures for the cut (start the light from the seat's current value) and for the A→B→A record. The pass-3 agglomeration retires B outright if P-A3 closes CK-03, CK-10's transient and CK-21 on the CSS path.

## P2-5 · The owner-glance packet (Q-1, Q-3, Q-5)

Every path is under `docs/tranches/BL/design/engagement/`. One line per frame pair. Chromium unless marked. Playwright WebKit paints no `backdrop-filter`, so its veil frames show the dim without the blur.

### Q-1 · engagement for a component nobody presses

The pass-1 plan asked pass 2 for one (a) frame beside (c) (`§P1-8`). **No pass-2 seat built the (a) variant**, so the (a) side comes from pass 1's R-C research build (10.1.0 + `R-C.patch`, a Separator inheriting `--engage`).

## P2-7 · Next-pass defect classes (recorded; no CK row added)

From the critiques and seat reports. Most are harness faults, which H3 cures inside the rows they already serve (CK-19, CK-20, CK-27). Those changes add no row.

1. **A witness films one channel and grades another.** E-10 records `x`/`y` and grades scale. A position plant is owed (C-E2 §4.1). Serves CK-20 and CK-27.
2. **Route seats edit the harness that grades them** (C-A2 §4.1). H3 rules the stateless programmatic commit (NOT DRIVEN or `element.click()`) and the page ground, each with a plant. A2's widened gallery moves into the harness app, so the latch reads on one app.
3. **E-8's PRM arm contradicts E-9** (CP §7.4, C-CP §4.1). Every kept clock reads as minted under reduce, A's authorized rows included.
4. **E-9's light leg reads the committed fill, not the transient** (C-CP CK-27). A passed with its commit light zeroed.
5. **The commit bound is finer than the read.** +0.002 against an alpha quantum of 1/255 = 0.0039 (C-CP §4.2). The accordion's `__light` saturates on a transparent face (CP §3.5).
6. **The capture's coarse "committed" is a 1200 ms long-tap that commits nothing** (C-CP §4.3, C-A2 §3). A short tap commits (LIT 12/12).
7. **The census counts a library-internal handler** (reka `Label`'s mousedown) as an interactive static (C-A2 CK-01).
8. **A shared pseudo-element claimed by a utility** (C-A2 §4.2). Scored this pass as CK-14's dock-slop counterexample; the class covers every `::after` user.
9. **The census reads a stamp, not a behaviour,** for classes whose recipe needs a writer (C-A2 §4.3).
10. **`ExpandableContainer` composes `engage-disclosed` on its whole root** (C-A2 §4.5, NOT-REPRODUCED).
11. **The noveil arm's open `backdrop-filter` plate**: compositor cost unmeasured (C-E2 §4.2).
12. **A pointerdown whose pointerup never arrives** (a context menu): `useHold` acquires on any button (C-E2 §4.3). UNMEASURED headless.
13. **A layout move with no DOM mutation** during a hold is not observed, so the control follows its anchor without the glide (C-E2 §4.4, P-E2 §7.5).
14. **E-5 sustain flips between reads** on the toggle and the accordion, as the Button's reduce squish does (C-A2 §4.4, C-E2 CK-06). One green read is not a cure.

The checklist stays **CK-01..CK-27, unchanged**. Critics may not add rows. A defect is a counterexample to the row it breaks, or a next-pass defect class. Defaults stand until the owner rules: Q-1 (c), Q-2 (b), Q-3 (c), Q-4 (b), Q-5 (a). Both Q-5 arms and both Q-3 keyboard arms are built again until ruled. A ruling that arrives mid-pass is folded in at the next seat boundary, never inside a running battery.

### P2-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`): at least three full passes; zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. Pass 3 is the last of the three required passes and can be the first clean one. The earliest convergence is pass 4, and only if the four owner-blocked items in §P2-6 are answered.

## P3-0 · In brief

| route | built as | critic | rows closed / applicable | pass 2 | status | the one-line reason |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| **D5-A** · the declared register | `P-A3-light.patch` (arm iii, default; contains F5′), `P-A3-geom.patch` beside it | C-A3 | **11/27 = 41 %** (CK-03, 08, 09, 11, 13, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26) | 22 % | **ADVANCE** | the census, the quiet arrival and the loud commit hold in both engines; the default commit still cannot be seen on three members, and its forced-colours arm deletes the dock trigger's hit-slop |
| **D5-E** · the engaged stage on A's table | `P-E3-veil.patch` and `P-E3-noveil.patch` (each F5′ + A3-light + E3) | C-E3 | **13/27 = 48 %** (CK-03, 04, 08, 09, 11, 12, 13, 15, 20, 21, 23, 24, 26) | 26 % | **ADVANCE** (partial by design) | the hand-off is cured on position in both engines and both keyboard arms; one MIGRATION row misstates the release path, and the composition inherits every open A row |
| **D5-B** · the WAAPI commit path | — | — | — | 4 % | **BANKED** (unchanged; §P3-2) | its re-trigger did not fire: A closed CK-03 and CK-21 on the CSS path |

**Pass 3 is not a clean pass.** Every route has open rows against CK-01..CK-27: A 16, E 14. The count of consecutive clean passes is **0 for every route**. Pass 3 is the third of the three passes the charter requires (`CHARTER.md` "Floors and fences"), so the pass floor is met; convergence now waits only on two consecutive clean passes, a fresh audit and the owner's four blocking answers (§P3-6). The earliest a route can converge is the end of pass 5, and only if pass 4 is clean, which needs those answers first.

Five findings shape pass 4.

1. **Pass 3's harness change worked as a gate.** H3's position-graded E-10 fails a shared-anchor plant at 68.0 px in both engines (C-E3 §0.1 `p-anchor`; H3 §3), and P-E3's per-footprint anchor names read 0.0 px outgoing and incoming centre moves on every hand-off path, both engines, both keyboard arms (P-E3 §3.1, C-E3 CK-20). Every plant both critics wrote failed its cell in both engines (C-A3 §1.3: P1, P3, P4, P6; C-E3 §1: p-anchor, p-char, p-script, p-tabhand, p-mod, p-scrubveil, p-vis, two unit plants). Pass 2's "green over work not done" did not recur inside the witnesses. It recurred once in a **probe** (finding 3) and once in a MIGRATION row (finding 4).
2. **The default commit still fails its own row, and only the owner can change that.** On the light arm, Switch (light), Radio (both themes) and the accordion (keyboard, programmatic, coarse) stay under the 0.01 bound in both engines (P-A3 §0.2, reproduced by C-A3 §0.3 and C-E3 CK-02). The geom build passes **164/164 in both engines** once arm ii is admitted (C-A3 §1.4), but it keeps **no** light transient under reduce on any member (E-9 60/18 both engines, C-A3 §1.4), so it trades CK-02 for CK-10. CK-10 on the light build is open on the same three members plus SegmentedTabs, which has no light seat (72/6 both engines, C-A3 CK-10). Q-5 remains the decisive ruling: either arm leaves a row open until it is ruled and the chosen arm is completed.
3. **One forced-colours arm reaches a pseudo-element it was never read on.** `utilities/engage.css:225-230` hides every `engage-disclosed` `::after` under forced colours, and the dock trigger is `engage-disclosed` through `SelectTrigger`, `PopoverTrigger` and `DropdownMenuTrigger` (`DockTrigger.vue:53-77`). The coarse dock trigger's 44 px slop becomes 0 × 0 and a tap 8 px above the face misses: 6/6 FAIL per engine with forced colours, 6/6 PASS without (C-A3 §0.2, replayed on E by C-E3 CK-14). P-A3's own CK-13 cell passed because it read only member seats (C-A3 CK-27, C-E3 CK-27). This is §P2-7 class 8 a second time, on the FL-3 arm.
4. **E's one new MIGRATION row is wrong about release.** E-M1 says a consumer `position-anchor` on an engaged Slider is overwritten while engaged; `stage.ts` `hide` also removes it after release, so `consumer anchor restored` FAILs in both engines and both arms (C-E3 §0.3). A wheel over an engaged control in an `overflow: auto` box scrolls the page (+30) and not the box (+0), in both engines, with no row (C-E3 §0.3). Both are CK-22 counterexamples.
5. **Three rows that pass 2 left unread are now read and fail.** CK-16: Skeleton and StatusDot keep their one infinite animation running 3000-4346 px off-screen in both engines (C-A3 §0.4, C-E3 CK-16). CK-06: E-5 sustain fails on some member on 3 of 3 Chromium reads at load 5.1-14.8 (C-A3 §0.4) and on 2 of 5 at load 6.9-9.9 (C-E3 §0.4), so it is not a load artefact; C-A3 reads it as one defect of the toggle's and accordion's shared press wash (identical 0.286 → 0.209). CK-19: the `ambient` exemplar's six frames are identical and E-1 reads 0.00 levels on a pointer move (both critics).

## P3-2 · Route entries

### D5-A · ADVANCE, 41 %

**What held** (C-A3 §0.1 and §2, replayed in both engines). The latch reads **54/55** on the harness's own app (pass 2: 25/55), with only `dock` unproven (D2's fence; no H3 scene renders it), and plant P1 drops it to 53/55. The committed mount is quiet 18/18 and the in-task arrival is quiet 14/14 on `pre`, `own` and `ownflush`, SegmentedTabs included; plant P3 (Checkbox without its door) fails `own` and `ownflush` by 0.0249-0.0326 (CK-03 CLOSED, F-7 extended to ten members by P-A3 §1.2). Every planted envelope token and `--rung-light` raises one `console.error` naming it on all eight members, 33/34 per engine; the one probe FAIL is the tabs clock, whose error names the resolved `translate` clock rather than the token, and both critics hold that the row asks for one error and gets it (CK-21 CLOSED). The probe also found and cured a masked fallback HEAD ships: SegmentedTabs' `parseFloat` read `calc(.21s * 1)` as NaN and fell to a 220 ms literal on every call (P-A3 §0.5). CK-14's pass-2 counterexample is cured: the seat no longer claims the dock trigger's `::after` (2/2 per engine; plant P4 fails it). Forced-colours and reduced-transparency arms are stated and read, 11/11 (WebKit's PRT cell is the engine's limit, CK-13 CLOSED). The numstat reproduces to the line (CK-23).

**Open rows, each with its counterexample** (C-A3 §2; rows E inherits are marked †):

## P3-8 · Pass-4 plan

The checklist stays **CK-01..CK-27, unchanged**. Critics may not add rows. A defect is a counterexample to the row it breaks, or a next-pass defect class. Defaults stand until the owner rules: Q-1 (c), Q-2 (b), Q-3 (c), Q-4 (b), Q-5 (a). Both Q-5 arms and both Q-3 keyboard arms are built again until ruled. A ruling that arrives mid-pass is folded in at the next seat boundary, never inside a running battery; each seat re-reads `../../audit/INBOUND.md` at its start.

**What pass 4 can and cannot be.** Without the five answers in §P3-6, pass 4 cannot be clean: the four owner-blocked rows and `dock`'s latch stay open. Pass 4 therefore has two jobs: close every row that can close by building, so that the only open rows are the five blocked ones, and leave both Q-5 arms complete enough that whichever the owner picks closes CK-02 and CK-10 together.

### P3-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 3 is 0 for every route. The earliest convergence is the end of pass 5, and only if the five answers in §P3-6 arrive before pass 4's critics run and pass 4 is clean.

## P4-0 · In brief

| route | built as | critic | rows closed / applicable | pass 3 | status | the one-line reason |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| **D5-A** · the declared register | `P-A4-light.patch` (arm iii, default; contains F5″), `P-A4-geom.patch` beside it | C-A4 | **13/27 = 48 %** (CK-03, 06, 08, 09, 11, 13, 14, 15, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26) | 41 % | **ADVANCE**, scored from pass 5 as the base of the composition (§P4-2) | the press now marks on `pointerdown`, the forced-colours arm keeps the dock slop, and the Radio dark commit paints; the cure that makes it paint stalls the light on a reverse and lands it past the acknowledge window |
| **D5-E** · the engaged stage on A's table | four arm builds, `P-E4-{noveil,veil}-{coarse,every}.patch` (each F5″ + A4-light + E3 + E4) | C-E4 | **16/27 = 59 %** by the critic (CK-03, 04, 06, 08, 09, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27); **14/27 = 52 %** on the composition (§P4-4: CK-12 and CK-27 reopen on A's carried bytes) | 48 % | **ADVANCE**; the composition A+E is the convergence candidate from pass 5 (§P4-2) | the anchor is given back, the wheel reaches its scroller and Q-3 (c) exists as a build, all four arms in both engines; a control that layout moved glides back from its old place on the next unrelated mutation |
| **D5-B** · the WAAPI commit path | — | — | — | — | **BANKED** (§P4-2) | its re-trigger did not fire again; its retirement now needs only the owner's Q-5 ruling toward arm ii, or CK-10 closed on the CSS path |

**Pass 4 is not a clean pass.** A has 14 open rows, E 11 by its critic and 13 on the composition. The clean-pass count is **0 for every route**. **The loop has not reached its fixed point:** besides the owner-blocked rows, seven rows still hold counterexamples that building can remove (§P4-6, last paragraph). The earliest convergence is the end of pass 6, and only if passes 5 and 6 are both clean, which needs the owner's answers in §P4-6 first.

Six findings shape pass 5.

1. **E-5 now reads green at its cause, and the cause was latency.** P-A4 filmed the held touch every 25 ms. Chromium sets `:active` 175-200 ms after touch-down, so E-5's +200 ms reference fell inside the wash's attack and read a ×0.73-0.89 "sag" (P-A4 §0.1). Keying the five light-press recipes on the door's `data-press` starts the wash at +25-50 ms. E-5 then reads 56/56 on three Chromium and two WebKit batteries (P-A4 §3 CK-06), and again on both critics' replays (C-A4 CK-06, C-E4 CK-06). **But E-5 cannot see the defect it was cured of.** C-A4's plant P7 puts the toggle back on `:active`, and it reads 3/3 sustain in both engines (C-A4 §0.5). The selectable Card still presses on `:active` and first shows at +200 ms in Chromium, 3/3 (C-A4 §1.4 `cardpress`).
2. **The Radio dark's cure is a delay, and the delay is a D-1 stall.** The fill-clock `transition-delay` (`control-bit.css`, `--engage-press-drain-release`, 120 ms) is what makes the Radio dark commit paint: C-A4's P9 removes it and the Radio returns to 0.0000-0.0001 on 5/5 paths in both engines. The same delay freezes the light 115-125 ms on a commit reversed within it, then blooms it on the face that has already gone back: 4/4 FAIL per engine; the no-delay control reads 0 ms frozen on 8/8 per engine (C-A4 §0.2). The commit's onset moves to +128-146 ms and its peak to +153-168 ms, past `ACKNOWLEDGE_WINDOW_MS` (150). C-A4 scores it at CK-20 and CK-12.
3. **The near-white "ceiling" is mostly a read, not the paint.** In paint (P-A4's `facefilm`) the Switch and Radio light faces rise 0.0140-0.0199 above rest after the fill in both engines, against the 0.01 bound (P-A4 §0.4). E-2's arm iii and E-9's `commit-light` still fail them at 0.0006-0.0064, because their composite reads a face's descendants (the Switch thumb, the Radio's 8 px dot) as whole-face layers (P-A4 §6.1). The accordion's light-theme face is a true ceiling in paint: 0.0000 excess, normal and under reduce, both engines (P-A4 §3 CK-02; C-A4 §1.4 `facefilm`). **The reverse also holds:** the toggle's dark commit light paints 0.0000 after the fill while E-2 passes it, and the tabs' dark light under reduce paints 0.0016-0.0018 while Chromium E-9 passes it (C-A4 §0.4, CK-27). The pass-2 claim that clearing 0.01 "needs about 25 % white" (`§P2-6`) now stands only for the accordion.
4. **The stage reads place only when something tells it to.** `stage.ts` `onMutation` glides each holder by `h.at − now`, and `h.at` refreshes on a `childList` mutation or a scroll only. A 120 px `style.height` move followed 400 ms later by an unrelated append plays a 127.2 px one-frame snap and a slide back, keyboard and in-place pointer paths, all four arms, both engines (124.7 px WebKit pointer); a viewport resize gives 21.9-25.0 px; the control reads 0.0 px (C-E4 §0.1). The promoted half is pass 3's code; pass 4 extended it to the in-place arm. H4's `re-render landing` and P-E4's `between landing` cannot fail for this cause, because each inserts the node that moves the control (C-E4 §4.2).
5. **Q-3 (c) now exists as a build, and the critics read it as the deft frame of the set.** `POINTER_STAGE = "coarse"` grows a fine pointer's hold ×1.060 in flow with no veil and no top layer, and promotes a touch's hold and the keyboard (P-E4 §0.3). E-3 reads 22/0 on all four arms in both engines; the four row plants and `pl-arm` fail as they must (C-E4 CK-04). Its consumer cost is real in one sibling: speedtest's `DashboardMapControls.vue:97` Card is an `overflow-hidden` ancestor of the slider at `:134`, so a fine hold grows under the clip there; on a fitting box the clip cell measures 10.8 px clipped in both engines (C-E4 CK-22, §4.3).
6. **The floor and the harness held.** H4 put six cells inside their rows, each with a plant, self-test green in both engines (H4 §0, §2). F5″'s F-10 stops Skeleton and StatusDot off-screen (+1001/+1002 ms/s → `paused`, both engines), and F-11 ends the secondary-button stick (F5pp §0.2-0.3). Every plant both critics wrote failed its cell in both engines, except C-A4's P7 (finding 1). Pass 3's records error did not recur: all seven patch hashes match their reports.

**The composition ruling (P-3, `§P1-6`).** `§P1-6` scores "the rows a route hands to another family's part … on the composition that carries them". Every E patch carries A's light bytes unchanged, so a counterexample C-A4 measured in those bytes is a counterexample on the composition, whichever critic read it. Two of C-E4's closures do not survive that: **CK-12** (the delayed light's attack, C-A4 CK-12) and **CK-27** (the toggle's dark commit and the tabs' dark reduce light pass witnesses while painting nothing; C-E4's own WebKit E-9 also passes the tabs dark, C-E4 CK-10). C-E4 did not read either; its CK-12 and CK-27 readings of E's own code stand. The composition stands at **14/27 = 52 %**, open on CK-01, 02, 05, 07, 10, 12, 16, 17, 18, 19, 20, 25, 27.

**Ruled: from pass 5 the convergence candidate is the composition A+E.** A and E were never incompatible routes: E is built on A's table and has been since pass 2 (`§P2-1`), and two of A's open rows (CK-04, CK-12's ladder half) exist only because A was scored alone. A stays the family that owns the commit, the press and the light; E owns the stage. Each critic scores all 27 rows on the composition's default build (`noveil-coarse` over `P-A5-light`), so the candidate gets two independent adversarial reads; the other arms are read beside it. If the owner rules Q-5 toward arm ii, E's delta is merged onto the geom build (it merged cleanly in passes 3 and 4) and that becomes the candidate.

The score fell from 52 % to 37 %. Pass 5 cured most of what it was sent to cure, but four rows reopened. CK-06 reopened because H5's sharper cell can now see latency, so it reads bytes that pass 4 passed. CK-11 and CK-14 reopened on shipped bytes that three passes had closed on a source comment, and CK-21 on a regression pass 5 made.

Six findings shape pass 6.

1. **Pass 5's targeted cures held where they were aimed.** The fill-clock stall is gone: the commit light is its own `ENGAGE_ENVELOPES` row (`commit-light`, 60/480 ms, retarget from current), its onset is +7..+22 ms, inside `ACKNOWLEDGE_WINDOW_MS` (P-A5, C-A5 §0.4, C-E5 §0.5), and C-A5's Q5 plant (the delay put back) fails 4/4 per engine. The disclosed press now deepens over its hover (CAP ladder PASS in both engines on both critics; accordion +0.0875 light and +0.0064 dark, C-E5 §0.5). The tabs' dark light under reduce paints (P8/Q8 fail at 0.0000 in both engines). The Card presses at +50 ms (Chromium) and +25 ms (WebKit), where it was +200 ms (C-A5 §1.4). The slider's press moved to `[data-held]`. On H5's cells the stale place reads 0.0 px per frame on all four arms in both engines, against pass 4's 127.2 px, and the `nowatch` plant brings the 127.2 px back (P-E5 §0.1, C-E5 §0.5).
2. **E's place observer has two blind spots, one finding per critic.** `stage.ts watchPlace` waits for an IntersectionObserver ratio to leave the value it was armed at. A move that leaves the ratio unchanged is never heard, and the next unrelated mutation glides the control from its stale place. (a) **A move inward:** the new rect lies inside the old one. On the fine in-place hold (Q-3 (c), the default) this plays a 63.6 px one-frame jump. Chromium 3/3 (loads 8-17); WebKit 1/1, plus a centred shrink that only WebKit fails (C-A5 §0.1). (b) **Inside a clipping scroller:** a move toward the visible side keeps the visible fraction. This plays 12.7 px on all four arms in both engines, 2/2 reads per arm. WebKit also fails the move away from the visible side, at 12.4-12.7 px (C-E5 §0.1). H5's stale-place cells drive only outward moves with no scroller, so neither blind spot could fail them. This is the third pass running in which E's place tracking has met a counterexample: pass 2's shared anchor, pass 4's mutation-only re-read, and now these two.
3. **The capsule's hover lift and press snap were never retired, and a library toggle still rides them.** `glass-capsule.css:122-131` keeps `.glass-capsule-hover:hover { scale: 1.015 }`, outside any `(hover: hover)` guard, and `:not([data-press-armed]):active { scale: var(--scale-press-sm, 0.97) }`. The Configurator's preset chip composes the class (`Configurator.vue:430`, an `aria-pressed` toggle), which this seat re-read at HEAD. On the candidate, C-A5 measures hover ×1.015, the press ×0.97, and a coarse tap that stays at ×1.015 at +900 ms with `:hover` true: 12/12 FAIL in both engines and both themes (`C-A5/logs/ca5-capsule-ca5app-cand-{chromium,webkit}.log`). Meanwhile `utilities/engage.css:14-16` says these are "retired at their source", and passes 2-4 closed CK-11 on that sentence. No scene renders the preset strip.
4. **A rename in the commit's loud read silenced the clocks the control bit still runs on.** P-A5 pointed `useEngage`'s `COMMIT_CLOCKS` at the new `commit-light` pair. But `control-bit.css` still transitions the bit's mark and face on `--engage-control-engage-{attack,release}`, and nothing now reads those two loudly. P-A3's `a3-probe ck21` plants `nonsense` on each and gets 0 `console.error` on 16/16 cells in both engines. Pass 4's read of the same probe was 33/34 (C-E5 §0.2, `C-E5/logs/xb-a3-probe-noveil-coarse-{chromium,webkit}.log`). This is a regression made in pass 5.
5. **Two Chromium-only defects reproduce across seats, and the slider carry is bimodal again.**
   - **The hand-off's scale drop** fails P-E5 5/14 and C-E5 2/3 (plus one read on noveil-every), with C-A5 not reproducing it on 1/1. WebKit passes on every arm. P-E5's `rafarm` variant (arm the observer one frame later) passed 4/4 in Chromium but was never read in WebKit, and it is not in the patches.
   - **A's +60 ms reverse** merges into 1 pulse on 1-5 cells in every Chromium read by all three seats (P-A5 gap 2, C-A5 §0.4, C-E5 §0.4). WebKit passes.
   - **The slider carry** fell under its 4 px bound on 4 of the pass's 14 Chromium reads: P-E5 3 of 9 (1.87-2.80 px, one at load 11.4) and C-A5 1/1 (2.72 px at load 10.5). C-E5 read 6.50-6.69 px on 3/3. WebKit reads 7.08-7.35 px. The low reads are not explained by load, and the spread is a mechanism question, not the margin question F-5 asks.
6. **O-74c G-1 is no longer the owner's.** O-103 (applied 2026-10-19 by the owner's accepted delegate, O-102b) placed atlas G-1 in **10.1.1**. The cure is `70d502ace`: `SheetContent.vue` takes the `fixed` utility and `sheet/styles.css` drops the losing `:where()` `position: fixed`. It shipped in `v10.1.1`, which is published (FORMATION-PROGRESS 2026-10-20 02:45). Master does not carry it yet, because the merge waits on the live design passes. So the Sheet cells of CK-01, CK-05 and CK-18 can now be built: the next floor carries the hunk, as F5 carried D4's PF-1 hunk.

### A+E · ADVANCE, 37 %

**What held**, with both critics reproducing each item in both engines: the latch at 54/55 (only `dock` short), with plants Q1/P1 and Q2/P2 failing; the committed mount and in-task arrivals quiet, with Q3/P3 failing; E-3 22/0 on all four arms, with the four keyboard plants each failing exactly their cells; one writer per attribute, with only `GlassDock.vue`'s `data-held` outside it (D2's, the named clause); one clock and no literal; forced colours on every `::after` the arm reaches, the dock slop kept, and WebKit's PRT the engine's limit; one engaged root per frame through the hand-off; the consumer anchor returned and the wheel handed to its scroller; cost reproduced to the line (182 files +4616/−1498; `src` 143 files +3005/−1237); `static` stamped and read; no lighting layer, and no veil on the candidate. Gates: build, typecheck and `demo:dist:build` exit 0; vitest 245 files, 2405 passed, 10 expected fail, 1 skipped (P-E5 §2, C-A5 §1.5, C-E5 §1).

**Open rows, each with its counterexample.** Rows marked ◇ cannot close by building. Unmarked rows can.

1. **CK-01.** ◇ The `dock` latch (D2). The Aurora `ambient` cell fails on hardware in both engines: 1.16-1.20 against 1.13-1.14 (H5 §3.3, C-A5 §1.1, C-E5 CK-01). The Sheet's PRM press has no target until G-1's hunk is in the base (finding 6).
2. **CK-02.** ◇ OPEN ON DEFAULT (Q-5). P-A5 trades the light-theme rise for the rebound. An early-onset light cannot rise over a fill that is darkening, so on the Switch, Checkbox, toggle and Chip light faces the light paints 0.0000 above the committed rest. The face falls from about 0.79 to 0.40 and absorbs the light (C-A5 §0.3). The accordion's light face reads 0.0000, normal and under reduce. Arm ii reads 164/164 in both engines (C-A5 §1.2).
3. **CK-05.** The bimodal slider carry (finding 5), which can be built. ◇ The WebKit Carousel needs the engine-limit clause. The Sheet can be built through G-1's hunk.
4. **CK-06.** The Button's gleam `::before` has NOT LANDED by +200 ms in either engine or theme (P-A5 gap 3, C-A5, C-E5). It rides `:active` and the `--press-t` spring. P-A5's r1 moved it to the mark and regressed, then reverted the move. H5's onset rule is what lets the cell see it now.
5. **CK-07.** ◇ OPEN ON DEFAULT (Q-2).
6. **CK-10.** Four faces fail in Chromium under reduce in paint: Switch light 0.0049-0.0090, checkbox light 0.0087-0.0098, toggle light 0.0073-0.0098, and chipaction dark 0.0061-0.0076. The toggle dark fails 3/3 Chromium reads at 0.0061-0.0080, which contradicts P-A5's "the same row answers the toggle dark's absorption" (0.0116-0.0117) (C-E5 §0.3, C-A5 CK-10). Under arm ii these stay open (geom E-9 73/5, C-A5 §1.2). ◇ The accordion's light face is Q-5 (2) under either arm.
7. **CK-11.** The capsule's lift and snap are live on the preset chip (finding 3).
8. **CK-12.** `commit-light`'s 480 ms release is sized by the witness. The row's own rider says three clocks "left the dark toggle at 0.0076 in paint, under the 0.01 bound", so four were chosen (`P-A5-light.patch:3759`). Even so, the four-clock figure does not clear that bound in Chromium (item 6).

10. **CK-16.** ◇ WebKit's `rest layouts` UNMEASURED (no counter); the engine-limit clause.
11. **CK-17.** ◇ Safari (§P5-6). The naming half can be built: the Button gleam, `glass/material.css:269-279`, `sortable-list/styles.css:141,197` and `dialog/styles.css:136` press on `:active` and name no `touchstart` listener (C-A5 CK-17). The dock triggers' `:active` belongs to D2.

## P5-8 · Pass-6 plan

The checklist stays **CK-01..CK-27, unchanged**. Critics may not add rows. A defect is a counterexample to the row it breaks, or a next-pass defect class. Defaults stand until the owner rules: Q-1 (c), Q-2 (b), Q-3 (c) as built, Q-4 (b), Q-5 (a). Both Q-5 arms and all four E arms are built again. A ruling that arrives mid-pass is folded in at the next seat boundary, never inside a running battery. Each seat re-reads `../../audit/INBOUND.md` and `../../FORMATION-PROGRESS.md` at its start. Every owner-gated row is recorded OPEN ON ITS DEFAULT.

**What pass 6 can be.** It cannot be clean without the answers in §P5-6 and D2's `dock` adoption. Its job is the same as pass 5's: remove every counterexample that building can remove, so that only owner- and D2-blocked cells remain.

**One change of method, for E's place.** E's place tracking has met a counterexample three passes running. P-E6 does not patch the observer cell by cell. It states the invariant once ("the stage hears every move of the rest box before the next frame it paints"), builds to it, and proves it against H6's whole cell family. If a construction cannot meet the invariant in both engines, P-E6 says so with the cell that fails.

### P5-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4`, `§P3-8.4`, `§P4-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 5 is **0**, counted for the one candidate A+E. Convergence comes at the end of pass 7 at the earliest. That needs three things:
- the answers in §P5-6 (Q-5 both parts, Q-2, the CK-17 scope line, the engine-limit form) arrive before pass 6's critics run;
- D2's `dock` adoption lands, or a dock scene renders with D2's leave;
- passes 6 and 7 are both clean.

Closed on the candidate: **CK-02, 03, 04, 06, 07, 08, 09, 10, 11, 13, 14, 15, 16, 18, 21, 23, 24, 26.** Open: **CK-01, 05, 12, 17, 19, 20, 22, 25, 27.**

**Pass 6 is not a clean pass. The clean-pass count stays 0.** **The loop has not reached its fixed point:** six open rows hold counterexamples that D5 can remove by building (CK-05, 12, 20, 22, 25, 27), and the ambient cell of CK-01/CK-19 still owes A's reduce leg. **No row is owner-gated any more**: O-103d answered Q-5, Q-2, the CK-17 scope and the engine-fact form. What cannot close inside D5 is D2's (`dock` latch, the dock's `:active` presses) and D3's (the Aurora's pointer amplitude). The earliest convergence moves to the end of pass 8, and only if passes 7 and 8 are both clean.

Six findings shape pass 7.

1. **The rulings closed four rows at once, and the arm-ii build holds them.** E-2 reads 164/0 in Chromium on both critics and in WebKit on C-E6 (C-A6's WebKit 160/4 under load re-read 32/0 twice). The PRM cell per face kind reads instant and legible on switch, checkbox, radio, tabs, toggle, chip and accordion in both engines (`a6-probe prm` 23/0, replayed by both critics). The commit light, its envelope row, its seat and AUTH-4 are deleted with no alias (P-A6). CK-07 closes on the S6 line; CK-16 and CK-05's Carousel take the engine-fact cell; CK-10's 18 E-9 `commit-light` cells test the retired form and are superseded, the presented fade surviving reduce on 16-23 frames (P-A6, C-A6, C-E6).
2. **E's place invariant holds, and one critic drove the one cell nobody could.** "The stage hears every move of the rest box before the next frame it paints": the IntersectionObserver is deleted and the rest box is re-observed each frame on one ResizeObserver, compared in the resize step (P-E6 §2). Every place cell in H6's family, `ca5-probe`'s 7, `ce5-probe`'s 6 keyboard cells and `e6-place`'s 4 pass on all four arms in both engines, on P-E6 and both critics. The `deaf` plant fails them; the `rafread` plant fails the late-frame move, which is why the read sits in the resize step. C-E6 built a driver for the pointer hold in a clipping scroller (clip by a spacer after the press, never a scroll under the hold): 6/6 per arm in both engines, and the deaf plant fails it 0/6 (C-E6 §0.1). The Chromium hand-off drop of pass 5 does not reproduce: 24/0 over 12 reads on the candidate, with a no-read control 10/0 (P-E6 §2), 8/8 on C-E6, 7/7 ×3 on C-A6.
3. **A new CK-20 counterexample, on every arm and in both engines: the promoted control escapes the card that clips its place, then vanishes in one step.** A keyboard engagement promotes the slider into the top layer. With its footprint 70 % clipped by its scroller, 14.6 px of the 21.2 px control paints over the card's edge; when the last pixel of the footprint leaves, `position-visibility: anchors-visible` drops the whole control in one 1 px scroll step. Painted-outside area per step at hidden 14..22 of 20 px: Chromium 3191 … 3976 → **0**, WebKit 2810 … 3589 → **0**, against a bound of 1074 px². Chromium 2/2, WebKit 1/1, and every other arm in Chromium (C-E6 §0.2). Pass 3 recorded the clip escape as engine-split (`§P3-7` item 4); it is now a discontinuity in both.
4. **Pass 6 cured the slider carry's bimodality at its cause, and the composition then thinned the WebKit margin.** F5‴ found two engine-dependent one-frame steps back at release (the transform origin declared only on the held rule; a `composite: "add"` carry on the channel the squish eases home on) and cured both: the floor alone reads min 8.64 px (Chromium) and 13.65 px (WebKit) on the held-frame reference, HEAD 0 of 5 (F5ppp §2 F-13). On the candidate the WebKit carry reads 4.35-7.44 px over 10 reads with one 0.00 at load 30.2, NOT-REPRODUCED 2/2 (C-A6 §1, §3 item 3), and 6.68-7.32 px on P-E6 and C-E6. E's merge drove the carry through `settleFrames(RELEASE_RULE.row, …)` (P-E6 §1). The cause of the halving is unread.
5. **The Sheet is graded for the first time, and its landing does not repeat.** F-12 put the Sheet where it should paint. It now presses (E-1 `runtime presented (sheet)` PASS on both critics, normal and PRM) and carries 125-250 px. But the same E-4 drive lands one or two detents apart: −160/−35/−35 and −285/−160/−160, with one fling carrying 0.00 px (C-E6 CK-05 s1, load 34.9). F5‴ read the split only at loads 28-38 and called it NOT-REPRODUCED off load; P-E6 then saw it at 13.7 and C-E6 at 17.4 on the same Sheet bytes. The cell prints no release velocity, so the drive's spread cannot be told from the rule's.
6. **A's part removed two counterexamples and made two.** The capsule left every non-dock composer, so CK-11 and CK-14 close (E-12 0 composers outside `dock/`; each critic's plant puts it back and fails 20 and 6 cells per engine). But the Configurator preset chip, an `aria-pressed` toggle, now changes **0.0 % of its pixels on hover and 0.0 % on a held press**, at ×1.0000, in both engines and both themes: no rule replaced what was removed (C-A6 §0, `ca6-probe chip`). That is the elegant-reduction trap CK-27 names, and a D-2 break. And P-A6 moved five public material classes' press from `:active::before` to `[data-press]::before` (`.glass-wash`, `-quiet`, `-resting`, `-floating`, `-overlay`) while `usePressMark` is not exported, so 89 consumer `.vue` lines (slides 54, fourier-analysis 22) lose their press with no MIGRATION row and no way back (C-A6 §0, CK-22).

### P6-8.2 · The floor

No floor seat unless one of two things happens before P-A7 starts:
- **the base moves** (master takes `release/10.1.x`): then F5⁗ is F5‴ rebased as `§P6-3` lays out (the Sheet pair drops, Skeleton takes F-14, the hold-token pair rebases with D2), with its battery on HEAD's scenes in both engines and F-14's browser cell;
- **H7's velocity cell shows the Sheet splitting at equal release velocity**: then F-15 cures the release rule's landing.

Otherwise F5‴ carries unchanged under every pass-7 build.

### P6-8.3 · Seats and order

Every seat is Opus, declared explicitly. D5 runs at most three seats at a time, counted against every live workflow. Browser runs follow the 2026-10-16 note: no `--light`, one browser per battery with a context per cell, `guarded --exclusive webkit` as the one WebKit lock. Each seat takes **one** WebKit acquisition for all its WebKit stages, queued after its Chromium phases and never skipped; where the 90-min cap forces more, each is queued and recorded. Films take no mid-film screenshot.

|---|---|---|

### P6-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4`, `§P3-8.4`, `§P4-8.4`, `§P5-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 6 is **0**, counted for the one candidate A+E. Convergence comes at the end of pass 8 at the earliest. That needs three things:
- D2's `dock` adoption and the dock's `:active` presses land, or a dock scene renders with D2's leave and its presses name their listener;
- D3's ambient amplitude lands, so CK-19 and CK-01's ambient cell can pass on hardware;
- passes 7 and 8 are both clean.

## P7-0 · In brief

| candidate | built as | critics | rows closed / applicable | pass 6 | status |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| **A+E** (A's pop, press and chip; E's stage, one arm) | `P-E7.patch` `a6c82db1…` = F5‴ + P-A6 + A7 + E's part with the veil and `"every"` arms deleted | C-A7 **21/27**, C-E7 **20/27**; ruled **21/27 = 78 %** (§P7-4) | 18/27 = 67 % | **ADVANCE** (not converged) |

Closed on the candidate: **CK-02, 03, 04, 05, 06, 07, 08, 09, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 18, 21, 23, 24, 26, 27.** Open: **CK-01, 17, 19** (held by D2 and D3) and **CK-20, 22, 25** (D5 can build).

**Pass 7 is not a clean pass. The clean-pass count stays 0.** **The loop has not reached its fixed point:** CK-20, CK-22 and CK-25 hold counterexamples that D5 can remove by building. What D5 cannot close is D2's (the `dock` latch, the dock's `:active` presses) and D3's (the Aurora's pointer amplitude, and now the lean's own scalar, which A's reduce leg needs). The earliest convergence moves to the end of pass 9, and only if passes 8 and 9 are both clean.

The score ran 52 % (pass 4, composition), 37 %, 67 %, 78 %. Six findings shape pass 8.

1. **A's part closed every row it was sent to close, and its bytes now hold no buildable open item.** CK-12 closes on both critics: `--scale-commit-pop: calc(2 - var(--scale-press))` (1.04, the press mirrored about rest) and `--rung-crest: var(--scale-engage)` (1.06, the engaged rung, for a command and the 20 px bit). The bit clause cites `control-bit.css:289-290`'s 0.8 px legible floor, which predates D5 (`155aa2f11`, 2026-08-08), and both critics read it as admissible (C-A7 §3 CK-12, C-E7 §3). The `crest` plant fails 8/8 per engine in paint and in the unit cell, and both crest inputs, planted `nonsense`, either keep the pop or kill it with one `console.error` (C-A7 §0.1). CK-27 closes: the Configurator preset chip is `ConfiguratorPresetChip`, an exported `chosen` member that answers hover at 3.78-5.23 levels mean and press at 46.5-50.7 % of its pixels by ≥ 16 levels, and `chipmute` fails H7's cell in both engines (C-A7 §3 CK-27). P-1 holds: the pop's reduce arm is one mechanism, and `prmY` fails 28 cells per engine (P-A7 §3, both critics). `usePressMark` is public on `/motion-core`, the Aurora's cursor has one writer, and P-A5 row 4 and P-A4 M-9 are retired (P-A7 §5). The Button commits visibly in a still (×1.0597-1.0606 against pressed ×1.032), and the coarse dark Switch press lightens the thumb (ΔL 0.0873 / 0.0862 against the pass-6 light references 0.0782 / 0.0773). The ambient reduce leg is the one A item not built, and it cannot be built in A's bytes: one uniform, `uCursorStrength`, drives both the swirl (geometry) and the lean (light), so any reduce write that lights the field also swirls it (P-A7 §6 item 1). It waits on D3.
2. **E's deletion is complete and the carry's halving is read to its cause.** `STAGE_VEIL`, `POINTER_STAGE`, the `"every"` branch, the stage veil and its CSS, and `flingStage` have 0 hits in `src` on both critics, against 17/6/12/4/6 on the pass-6 patch (C-E7 §1, C-A7 §1). E's `src` line is 51 lines smaller than in pass 6 after adding the clip (P-E7 §1). The WebKit halving was `flingStage`: a fine-pointer hold is a holder, so every slider release took the stage stretch, which moves the value point only by its fraction of the host's width. At matched release velocity the stretch carries 0.54-0.60 of the body carry in both engines, reproduced by both critics (P-E7 §3; C-E7 §3 CK-05; C-A7 §2 `fling6`). With the route deleted, every slider read whose release the rule measured above 0 px/s carries ≥ 5.95 px across three seats and both engines (§P7-3). **F-5 is askable** (§P7-6).
3. **CK-20: the clip cures the overflow rectangle and nothing else.** H7's clip-vanish cell and P-E7's touch cell read 0 px² on every step in both engines, and `clip6` brings back the pass-6 figures to the px² (P-E7 §3, both critics). But `clipBox` reads only ancestors' `overflow` boxes and paint containment, and the library paints other clips. C-E7 drove four (C-E7 §3 CK-20):
   - the library's own feather (`.fading-scroll`, the Configurator's controls column): promoted, the control paints at ×1.46 (Chromium) and ×1.39 (WebKit) of its in-flow visibility, against ×1.05 / ×1.00 with the feather off. fourier-analysis renders a glass `<Slider>` in that column (`VisualizationView.vue:459`, `EquationView.vue:495`);
   - a 40 px rounded scroller: 221 px² (Chromium) and 201 px² (WebKit) painted in the corners, against a 16 px² bound;
   - a scaled ancestor (`scale: 0.8`): the control jumps 52.2 px (Chromium) and 28.7 px (WebKit) in the frame it promotes, and paints at ×0.848 of its in-flow width. A translated ancestor reads 0.0 px;
   - a wheel scroll in Chromium: one frame of 730 px² unclipped paint above the card in 5 of 9 engaged films (203 px² in a sixth), 0 of 9 released films, against `clip6`'s 731-866 px². WebKit is clean.
4. **CK-25: CK-20's cure cuts the loupe.** A touch hold promotes the slider and the loupe rises 80.6 px above it. The clip now cuts the loupe at its scroller's edge: 93 % of it with the control 4 px below the top edge, 40 % mid-box in the 120 px `clipstale` scroller, in both engines (C-A7 §0.6, `c7-probe cliploupe`). Every loupe cell P-E7 ran was mid-page, so the cost could not show there. The loupe at `k` max passes E's four cells per engine and `loupe6` fails them, and both critics read it as better than pass 6: a window onto a magnified strip, not a split pill. Neither reads it as a lens yet: the fill still ends in its own round pill cap at the window's centre, which reads as a knob, and the housing has no lens edge (C-E7 §3 CK-25, C-A7 §3 CK-25).
5. **The promoted body now buys only stacking, and E's promotion has met a place or clip counterexample five passes running.** Pass 2: one shared anchor (68 px teleport). Pass 4: place re-read only on mutation (127.2 px glide). Pass 5: the observer's inward and clipped blind spots. Pass 6: the escape over the card and the one-step vanish. Pass 7: every clip that is not an overflow rectangle, a scaled ancestor, a scroll lag, and the loupe cut. P-E7's clip makes a promoted body paint what its place would paint. A body that paints what its place paints is what the in-place grow already gives, with no top layer, no per-frame clip write and no anchor to lag. The one part of an engaged slider meant to leave its place is the loupe (C-A7 §4, "the clip should bound the control's body, not the loupe"). The charter asks for a fresh formulation when a pass stalls ("Portfolio management"). §P7-8 states the invariant once for the ruled construction, measures the fresh formulation beside it without scoring it, and §P7-6 holds the Q-3 question for the owner until both are measured.
6. **CK-22: A's side is complete; E's side is not.** A's rows M-12..M-22 reproduce by both critics' greps at HEAD (slides 54, fourier-analysis 22, value.js 3 material-class lines; `:active` on a material class in consumer source 0; one pressable site, slides `DeckSettings.vue:149`, named in M-12). E retired none of its own stale rows. P-E2 §4 rows 1, 2 and 4, E-M7's `"every"` clause and E-M8's `true` arm describe deleted bytes. M-24 promises a promoted slider "stays inside the scroller or card it sits in, as it does at rest", which finding 3 shows false on a feathered, rounded or scaled card (C-E7 §3 CK-22, C-A7 §3 CK-22). C-A7 also finds M-20 counts the wrong population: it lists `setCursor` callers, while the surface it migrates is the `<Aurora>` root. The three `<Aurora>` mounts (atlas `Atmosphere.vue:137`, speedtest `App.vue:48`, keyframes.js `HeroAurora.vue:25`) are all `pointer-events: none`, so the root leg never fires in any consumer. "Nothing changes for them" holds, but for that reason (C-A7 §3 CK-22, a note).

The checklist stays **CK-01..CK-27, unchanged**. Critics may not add rows. A defect is a counterexample to the row it breaks, or a next-pass defect class. Otherwise its job is the fixed point: CK-20, CK-22 and CK-25 closed, so that only CK-01, CK-17 and CK-19 stay open, each held by D2 or D3.

The fresh formulation is built beside it as a measured probe, not a candidate, so the owner can rule Q-3′ on two measured constructions. Critics score the candidate alone.

### P7-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4` .. `§P6-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 7 is **0**, counted for the one candidate A+E. Convergence comes at the end of pass 9 at the earliest. That needs four things:
- D2's `dock` adoption and the dock's presses land, or a dock scene renders with D2's leave and its presses name their listener;
- D3's amplitude and the lean's own lane reach D5's base (through master or F-16), and A's reduce write lands on them;
- passes 8 and 9 are both clean.

If the owner rules Q-3′ toward the fresh formulation at pass 8's close, it becomes the candidate in pass 9, the count restarts on it, and the earliest convergence moves to the end of pass 10.

| candidate | built as | critics | rows closed / applicable | pass 7 | status |
