**Pass 8 is not a clean pass. The clean-pass count stays 0.** **The loop has not reached its fixed point:** CK-20, CK-22 and CK-25 each hold a counterexample that D5 can remove by building. Convergence moves to the end of pass 10 at the earliest, whichever way the owner rules Q-3′ (§P8-8.4).

The score ran 52 % (pass 4), 37 %, 67 %, 78 %, 78 %. The count did not move, but the rows under it did: CK-25's lens items and every cell H8 wrote now pass, and the counterexamples moved to ancestor kinds and paths no earlier pass drove. Six findings shape pass 9.

1. **P-E8 meets the invariant on every cell H8 wrote, in both engines, and the pass-7 construction fails them.** E-10 58/0 per engine on P-E8, C-A8 and C-E8 (C-E8's one WebKit film fail at load 16.8 re-read 0 of 2 in a second acquisition, NOT-REPRODUCED; C-E8 §3 CK-20). On `plant-cand7` 7 of 14 clip cells fail in Chromium and 6 of 14 in WebKit, reproduced by all three seats. E-13 `loupe whole` reads 81 of 81 rows at the scroller's edge and mid-box, 4/4 per engine (pass 7: 3-5 and 48 of 81). `e8-probe tip` reads spread 0.00 px, 4/4 per engine (pass 7's pill cap 3.60-3.87 px). `position-visibility: always`, the anchor pin and `@property --veil-x/--veil-y` are deleted (`static.sh`, P-E8 and C-A8). E-2 164/0, E-3 22/0, E-9 85/0 and E-4 with velocity printed hold on both critics.
2. **Off H8's cells the invariant breaks on every ancestor kind the stage does not enumerate.** The stage re-derives its place's paint for the overflow box, radius, mask, scale and translate. Both critics built the kinds it leaves out, two-sided, against the in-place probe on the same cells (C-A8 §0.4, C-E8 §3 CK-20):

   | ancestor | C-A8 Chromium / WebKit | C-E8 Chromium / WebKit | probe (in place) |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `opacity: 0.5` | ×1.966 / ×1.984 | ×1.960 / ×1.984 (keyboard) | 0.99-1.03 |
   | `clip-path: inset(…)` | ×1.475 / ×1.472 | ×1.406 / ×1.400 (keyboard) | 0.98-1.02 |
   | `rotate: 3-4deg` | ×2.202 / ×2.554 | ×1.877 / ×2.156 (keyboard) | 1.00-1.12 |
   | `filter: invert(1)` | not built | ×0.227 / ×0.222 (keyboard) | 1.04 / 1.10 (WebKit's in-place filter reads scatter to 1.38) |

   Under rotation `paintedScale` divides a rotated footprint's bounding box by its layout box, so the body is drawn unrotated and about twice its height (C-A8 reads the host's `matrix(1.0015, 0, 0, 2.2462, 0, 0)`). `clip-path` and rotation are inside the invariant's own words ("every clip … and transform"). Opacity is the library's own path: **a Slider keyboard-engaged in the library's Dialog, then dismissed, stays promoted on its exit clock at opacity 1 over the fading plate and drops onto it in one frame**, step 0.73-0.90 in both engines (C-A8 `dlg`; the probe 0.00). That is a D-1 discontinuity on a library path.
3. **Two of P-E8's own changes regressed a pass-7 reading.**
   - **The scroll trail (Chromium).** Deleting the anchor moved the body onto the stage's resize step, one compositor frame behind its place. C-A8: 2.7-3.0 px on 9 of 9 engaged films (wheel over, wheel elsewhere, page scroll), against ≤ 0.5 px for the pass-7 construction and the probe. C-E8: 4 px in 1-2 frames of 5 of 6 films; the probe 0 px. H8's film cell reads only paint escaping above the card, and clip and body move together, so it passes. WebKit's 25 fps film is no one-frame witness (H8 §5), and C-A8's WebKit trail instrument is confounded (§P8-7 item 9).
   - **The loupe under a scaled card.** The popover lens carries the host's grow (`--held-scale-*`) but not its ancestors' scale: it magnifies **×2.000, not k max ×1.600**, both engines and pointers, and in Chromium the fine lens floats **39.4 px above its track** (C-E8 §3 CK-25). The pass-7 lens, the host's child, read ×1.600 and 0.0 px on the same cells. The probe shares the candidate's lens and fails the same way.
4. **Two more place counterexamples, each on one critic's path.** C-A8's `glidescale`: a re-render glide under a `scale: 0.8` scroller starts 20 % short (5.25 / 5.42 px in the insertion's frame against 1.64 / 1.52 unscaled), because `glide()` composites `translate` after the inline `scale(0.8)`; the probe 1.43 / 1.22. And the WebKit keyboard feather at ×0.58 (P-E8 §0.3), read four times by C-A8 and at ×0.566 by C-E8: **C-A8 reads 0.584 with the focus ring suppressed, so the focus-ring hypothesis is refuted**, and C-E8's frame shows the engaged track's rim and shadow gone and its pill ends cut flat. The cause is unmeasured; the frame is consistent with the mask clipping to the border box. H8's feather bound is one-sided (≤ 1.10), so the under-paint passes it. Chromium reads 1.02, WebKit touch 0.98, the probe 0.99.
5. **The loupe is whole above a card but not at the viewport's top.** With the card at the viewport's top and the rest box 4 px below its edge, 90-92 % of the lens lies above the viewport, unpainted, in both engines and pointers, in **every** build: pass 7, the candidate and the probe (C-E8 `loupe viewport`). The lens is hung `bottom: anchor(top)` with no `position-try`. The viewport is the root scroller, so this is inside CK-25's pass-8 clause ("paints whole at a scroller's top edge"). The lens clauses otherwise close: no pill cap (tip 0.00 px), the housing's own hairline (both critics see it, C-A8 calls it faint), no lighting layer, no blur token, a flat window both critics read as admissible under O-87.
6. **The promotion has now met a place or paint counterexample six passes running, and the in-place probe meets every one of them by construction.** Pass 2 one shared anchor; pass 4 a stale place on mutation; pass 5 the observer's blind spots; pass 6 the escape and the one-step vanish; pass 7 every clip but the overflow box; pass 8 every ancestor kind but five, the Dialog's fade, the compositor's scroll clock and its own glide. Both critics read the probe beside the candidate. On every pass-8 counterexample the probe reads in place: opacity, `clip-path`, rotation, filter, the Dialog (0.00), the WebKit feather (0.99), the trail (≤ 0.5 px), the glide under scale (1.43 / 1.22). It removes 422 lines of E's `src` and the engaged idle loop (0.0/s against 120-133 and 98). Its one red set is 16 stale-place cells, the re-render glide's (E-10 42/16 per engine, all three seats). P-E8 measured the probe with the per-frame read kept (`E8/probe-loop-variant.patch`): **E-10 58/0 per engine**, at the candidate's idle cost (129 / 98 per s); neither critic re-ran that variant. What the probe gives up is one stacking fact: a fine pointer's in-place grow sits in its own stacking context, so a later positioned sibling can paint over it, and on touch and keyboard the same now holds. No cell reads that, in either build. **Q-3′ is asked now** (§P8-6).

## P8-6 · What the owner must rule, and what D2 and D3 hold

### Q-3′ · asked now, as one message

> **Q-3′ (D5, engagement).** Pass 8 measured two constructions of the engaged Slider and NumberField on the same cells, both engines (Chromium 149, Playwright WebKit 26.5; real Safari unmeasured).
>
> **(c), as ruled (O-103d (5)), the candidate:** a fine pointer grows the control in place; a touch hold or a keyboard engagement promotes it into the top layer, and each frame the library re-draws its place's paint on it by script (clip, rounded corners, `FadingScroll` mask, translate, scale). It passes every cell the harness wrote. It fails, in both engines, on every ancestor property it does not copy: a half-faded card (the body at full strength, ×1.96-1.98), an ancestor `clip-path` (×1.40-1.48), a rotated card (drawn unrotated and about twice its height), a filter; and on three paths: the library's own Dialog dismissed under an engaged Slider (a one-frame opacity drop of 0.73-0.90), a one-frame trail behind its place on every Chromium scroll (2.7-4 px), and a re-render glide 20 % short under a scaled card. In WebKit its keyboard body under a feather loses its rim (×0.58). It is the sixth pass whose promotion met a place counterexample. Its E code is 422 lines larger, and it runs a frame loop while engaged (120-133 callbacks/s Chromium, 98 WebKit; 0 layouts).
>
> **In place, the probe:** the control grows where it stands under every modality, with no top layer, no script copy of its place and no anchor; the loupe alone leaves its place. It paints exactly as its place on every case above, both engines, by construction (0.97-1.03 on the ancestor kinds; Dialog 0.00; trail ≤ 0.5 px; feather 0.99). Without a per-frame read it fails 16 cells where a re-render glides from a place a layout move left stale (up to 127 px); with the per-frame read kept it passes all 58 of E-10 in both engines (measured by its builder only so far) at the candidate's idle cost. What it gives up: an engaged control no longer rises above a later positioned sibling; it grows in its own stacking context, as a fine-pointer hold already does today. No cell reads that difference.
>
> Both builds share the loupe, which still needs two cures either way (it over-magnifies under a scaled card, and is cut off at the viewport's top).
>
> **Rule one:** (c) keep promoting on touch and keyboard, and pass 9 makes the script copy carry every ancestor kind and the scroll clock; or **(i) in place under every modality**, the loupe alone promoted, and pass 9 builds the probe with the per-frame read as the candidate. Default until you answer: (c). The convergence date is the same either way (end of pass 10 at the earliest), because the clean-pass count is 0.
>
> **F-5, still open:** keep the slider carry's bound at "fling minus still ≥ 4 px" (a, the default), or tie it to the measured release velocity (b). Every pass-8 read clears (a) by ≥ 7.2 px.

The measured record, stated plainly and not as a ruling: on every counterexample of passes 7 and 8 the in-place construction holds and the promoted one does not, and it is the smaller one (P-1). The one thing the promotion still buys is the stacking fact, which no row and no cell asks for. The owner rules.

### P8-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4` .. `§P7-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 8 is **0**, counted for the one candidate A+E. Convergence comes at the end of pass 10 at the earliest, and needs:
- D2's `dock` adoption and the dock's presses, or a dock scene with D2's leave and its presses naming their listener;
- D3's amplitude and the lean's own lane in D5's base (through master or F-16), with A's reduce write on them;
- passes 9 and 10 both clean.

If the owner rules Q-3′ "in place", the in-place construction is the candidate from pass 9 and the count starts on it at 0. Since the count is 0 now, that ruling moves the earliest convergence by nothing. If the owner rules it after P-E9 has started, it is folded in at pass 10's first seat boundary and the earliest convergence moves to the end of pass 11.

## P9-0 · In brief

| candidate | built as | critics | rows closed / applicable | pass 8 | status |

**Pass 9 is not a clean pass. The clean-pass count stays 0**, counted on the in-place candidate from this pass. **The loop has not reached its fixed point:** CK-20 and CK-25 each hold a lens counterexample D5 can remove. Convergence moves to the end of pass 11 at the earliest (§P9-8.4).

The score ran 52 % (pass 4), 37 %, 67 %, 78 %, 78 %, 81 %. Six findings shape pass 10.

1. **The in-place construction holds every pass-8 counterexample, in both engines, on both critics.** E-10 72/0 Chromium, 69/0 WebKit; E-13 16/0 per engine, on P-E9 and both critics. Ancestor opacity 1.009 / 1.032 and 1.001 / 0.989, `clip-path` 1.003 / 1.024 and 1.010 / 0.980, rotation and filter on their graded modes inside 0.90..1.10, the Dialog's step equal to the plate's within its 0.05 bound, the Chromium place film 0 px, the glide under scale 0.46-0.48 px Chromium, the feather two-sided at 0.991-1.005 (C-A9 §0.3, C-E9 §5). C-E9 also read skew, mask, zoom, perspective and `mix-blend-mode` (§4): all inside the bound but WebKit's blend at 1.102, an in-place body's scatter. The promotion's six-pass run of place counterexamples has ended, because nothing leaves its place but the loupe. The cost fell: `src` +3088/−1359 against pass 8's +3380/−1355, reproduced to the line by both critics.
2. **O-103f's conditions, read by non-builders.** (1) The idle cost sits at the bar on two independent re-measures: one rAF and one ResizeObserver callback per frame on both builds, 0 layouts, 0 recalcs (C-A9 §0.5; C-E9 §2). (3) The loop stops at release: 0.0/s from the release on, 5/5 release paths per engine (C-A9), the plant red at 98.9-106.7 per s (C-A9) and 52-105 per s (C-E9). (4) Real Safari stays UNMEASURED. (2) Both loupe cures land on the settled lens (×1.600, 0.0 px, 81 of 81 rows, both engines), and finding 4 is what they left behind.
3. **CK-22 closes.** One table, C-1..C-30, from pass 1; `pass-1/P-A.md` §7, `P-B.md` §7, `P-E.md` §7 and `F5.md`'s lines each carried or retired by name, checked source by source by C-A9 (§1.1); the consumer counts reproduced at HEAD by two independently written `grep -e` scripts (C-A9 `ck22-greps.txt`, C-E9 `ck22-greps-*.txt`). Two wording items go to P-E10 (§P9-4).
4. **Every open D5 item is in the lens, each with one named cause, and each is small.**
   - **The side swap (CK-20).** `placeLens` decides the side from the track's position each frame (`below = t.top − lens.offsetHeight × s.y < 0`, P-E9 patch line 8133) and flips it with no travel. A held lens crossing the line moves 101.8 px (Chromium) and 101.7 px (WebKit) against its track in one frame (C-A9 §0.2, C-E9 §1.2). ±3 px about the line flips it 20 times in 41 samples, both engines (C-E9 `flipjitter`).
   - **The lens off its track on its own clocks (CK-25).** Under a scaled card in Chromium the lens's `scale` property composes outside its `transform`, so the raise's and the exit's own scale multiply the correction `--lens-dx/-dy`: up to 10.9 px off on the raise and up to 34.3 px on the frozen exit, with 16.9 px at opacity 0.51 in one exit (C-E9 §1.3). `plant-cause` moves the correction into the `translate` property and reads 0.0 px on every clock cell. E-13 could not see this: it reads a hold sustained 1200 ms.
5. **All of finding 4 is shared with the probe, so none of it bears on Q-3′.** C-A9 §0.2 and §2, C-E9 §1.2 and §5.
6. **The rows D2 and D3 hold have no wave that builds them.** `../../waves/W-0.1.md` and `W-0.8.md` name none of CK-01, CK-17, CK-19, `touchstart`, the lean or D5 (0 hits at this seat). D2 is at its confirmation pass and D3 has converged. Unless formation adds them, D5 reaches its fixed point and stops there (§P9-6).

A ruling that arrives mid-pass is folded in at the next seat boundary, never inside a running battery. Each seat re-reads `../../audit/INBOUND.md` and `../../FORMATION-PROGRESS.md` at its start. The candidate is the in-place construction; there is no second construction to carry.

**What pass 10 can be.** It is clean only if D2's and D3's held cells reach the base before its critics run, which needs §P9-6's addendum first. Otherwise its job is the fixed point: CK-20 and CK-25's lens items closed, so that only CK-01, CK-17 and CK-19 stay open, each held by D2 or D3.

### P9-8.2 · The floor

No floor seat unless one of three things happens before P-E10 starts: the base moves (F5⁗ = F5‴ rebased as `§P6-3` lays out, its battery on HEAD's scenes in both engines and F-14's browser cell; the 10.1.3 lines rebase with it); D3 publishes the lean lane (F-16 carries the hunk, and P-A10 runs); D2 lands its dock adoption, or publishes a dock scene with its leave (F-17, which D2's patch can supply without waiting for master once D2 converges). Otherwise F5‴ carries unchanged. If the base moves mid-pass, it is folded in at the next seat boundary.

### P9-8.3 · Seats and order

Every seat is Opus, declared explicitly. D5 runs at most three seats at a time, counted against every live workflow. Browser runs follow the 2026-10-16 note: no `--light`, one browser per battery with a context per cell, `guarded --exclusive webkit` as the one WebKit lock. Each seat takes **one** WebKit acquisition for all its WebKit stages, after its Chromium phases, never skipped, with re-read stages queued inside it, and one waiter per chain. Films take no mid-film screenshot. Runners are replaced by rename, never edited in flight.

### P9-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4` .. `§P8-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 9 is **0**, counted for the one candidate A+E, in place. Convergence comes at the end of pass 11 at the earliest, and needs:
- P-E10's lens items closed on both critics;
- D2's `dock` adoption and the dock's presses, or a dock scene with D2's leave and its presses naming their listener, in D5's base;
- D3's amplitude and the lean's own lane in D5's base (through master or F-16), with A's reduce write on them;
- passes 10 and 11 both clean.

If the held cells reach the base after pass 10's critics run, each pass of delay moves that date by one pass. The count cannot start until they land.

Under `zoom: 0.8` it reads 16.3 px against 12.3 (both critics, both engines, to the decimal). Under `zoom: 1.25` it reads 31.8-32.5 against 18.7 (C-E10, its 16.7 ms window) and 41.2-43.1 against 23.1 (C-A10, per frame), with the lens 52-54 px above the viewport mid-change (both). Under `zoom: 2` it never changes side and sits 40-42 px out of the viewport; under `zoom: 2.5` the clock cell's correction diverges to the layout coordinate limit (C-A10 §0.5). **One cause, read by both critics and confirmed in the bytes at this seat:** `placeLens` reads painted px (`getBoundingClientRect`, `paintedScale`) and writes `--lens-dx/-dy` and the travel's span in the lens's CSS px, which an inherited `zoom` scales; `paintedScale` walks `transform` and `scale`, not `zoom`, and `offsetHeight` is unzoomed, so the side test mixes frames too. No sibling sets CSS `zoom` (C-A10 `logs/ck20-zoom-sites.txt`), and the plan named `zoom` as a path to drive (`§P9-8.3` wave 3). It is CK-20's, D5-buildable and small.

## P10-8 · Pass-11 plan

The checklist stays **CK-01..CK-27, unchanged**. Critics may not add rows. Rulings in force: O-103d (1)-(4); O-103f; Q-1 (c) and Q-4 (b) as defaults; Q7 on its default. The candidate is the in-place construction; there is no second construction to carry and no probe.

**What pass 11 can be.** It is clean only if D2's and D3's held cells reach D5's base before its critics run (F-17 needs D2 converged, which is the end of D2's pass 11 at the earliest; F-16 needs D3's hunk). On the present record that will not happen, so pass 11's job is **the fixed point**: CK-20 and CK-21 closed on both critics, so that only CK-01, CK-17, CK-19 and CK-25's ambient clause stay open, each held by D2 or D3. If pass 11 reaches it, **D5 runs no further build pass until the held cells land**; its next pass is then the first confirmation pass on the base that carries them.

Every seat is Opus, declared explicitly. D5 runs at most three seats at a time, counted against every live workflow. ### P10-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4` .. `§P9-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 10 is **0**, counted for the one candidate A+E, in place. Convergence comes at the end of pass 12 at the earliest, and needs:
- P-E11's CK-20 and CK-21 items closed on both critics;
- D2's `dock` adoption and the dock's presses, or a dock scene with D2's leave and its presses naming their listener, in D5's base (W-0.8 §10, F-17);
- D3's amplitude and the lean's own lane in D5's base (through master or F-16), with A's reduce write on them (W-0.1 addendum);
- passes 11 and 12 both clean.

On the present record pass 11 cannot be clean (§P10-8), so the realistic earliest is two confirmation passes after the held cells land, which follows D2's convergence (end of its pass 11 at the earliest) and the 10.2.0 landings of W-0.8 and W-0.1. Each pass of delay moves that date by one pass. The count cannot start until the held cells land.

2. **CK-21 stays open on one shared counterexample: an easing list passes the check and throws.** `glide` asks `CSS.supports("animation-timing-function", easing)`, a property that takes a list of timing functions; `animate()`'s `easing` takes one. `--spring-dock: ease, linear` (and `linear(0, 1), ease` / `linear(0, 1), ease-in`) reads `supports = true` and throws uncaught from `animate()` on **both** the lens's side change and the host's re-render glide in `onMutation`, with 0 errors naming the token, both engines, both critics (C-A11 §0.1, C-E11 §0.5 (a)); library-free first (C-A11 `easing-facts.mjs`). The loop survives: a later hold engages at 1.00 and lays its lens at 0.0 px (both). Each critic's control asks the parser `animate()` uses (`new KeyframeEffect(null, null, { easing })` in a `try`): C-A11's `listcure` 14/14 per engine, C-E11's `waapicure` all four list cells green per engine. The cells P-E11 was sent to cure hold: `nonsense` on either path, `initial`, `--spring-dock-settle: nonsense` and `-1`, `var(--undefined)`, loud by name, no throw (both).
3. **CK-21's second counterexample, C-E11's: a settle token written in `ms` is read as seconds.** `--spring-dock-settle: 210ms` is a valid `<time>`. `rowClockMs` does `parseFloat(…) × 1000` and reads 210 000 ms, so the lens's travel, the host's glide and the slider's release carry (`useEngage.ts` `carry()`, `RELEASE_RULE.row` `dock`) all run 210 s with **0 errors**, both engines (C-E11 §0.5 (b)). The stylesheet reads the same token correctly through `calc(var(--spring-dock-settle) × tempo)` (`scheme-spring.css:110`), so CSS and script disagree 1000× on one clock, silently. That is E-2's silent degradation and CK-21's text ("every token … fails loud when unreadable", and a misread is worse than unreadable). C-E11's `unitcure` reads the unit and turns the three cells green per engine. C-A11 did not drive a unit-bearing value; nothing refutes it (§P11-4). The function is one: it came from P-A7's `useEngage.ts` (`P-A7.patch:7157`) and lives in the candidate in `settle.ts`, which E's patch introduced, imported by the stage and the carry (`P-E11.patch:8522`), so one E edit cures all three paths; no A seat is needed.
4. **CK-20 stays open on one shared counterexample: the host's re-render glide under an ancestor `scale`, for a move outside the scaled box.** Both critics insert or remove a block above a `scale: 0.8` card while its slider is held. The host's painted place jumps on the mutation's frame by the move × (1 − 0.8): **8.0 px for C-A11's 40 px move, 12.0 px for C-E11's 60 px**, then glides the rest; the move over 16.7 ms reads 7.0-8.5 px against 6.5 (C-A11) and 12.0-12.5 against 9.3 (C-E11). Both engines, both critics, at every load they read it; also with the scale one box further out (C-A11 `nested`). Untransformed, under `zoom` 0.8, 1.25 and 2, and for a move inside the scaled card: 0.0 px (both). **One cause, read by both critics and confirmed in the bytes at this seat:** `flowBox` brings each offset into the host's px by the zoom ratio alone (`P-E11.patch:8137`); an offset of a box outside the scaled card is in page px, while the glide's `translate` paints at the host's scale. P-E11 §1's argument ("a transform maps an offset and a `translate` alike") holds for an offset inside the transformed box, not above it. C-A11's `glideframe` (each offset through `frameOf(e).y / frameOf(host).y`) reads 5/5 per engine at 0.0 px. P-E11 §1 names the risk a full-frame read carries: a running ancestor scale animation read as a moved place, glided by the next unrelated mutation. `glideframe` reads `frameOf`, which reads the computed transform, so that risk applies to it as written; the cure must answer it with a cell (C-E11 §4 item 3, C-A11 §0.2).
5. **CK-12 and CK-22 close on both critics, with each overclaim counted once.** CK-12: P-E11's band paragraph (the band and `HOLD_SLOP_PX` are the same quantity, hand and world parting in viewport px) answers C-A10's remark; no figure minted; both accept it. CK-22: C-2 reads "The stage writes only `--stage-room` and `--stage-w`", reka's var named as reka's, true against the bytes and the DOM in both critics' reads; C-9's zoom clauses true. Two sentences are false on these bytes, each the open defect's and counted there (P-3): C-9's "an easing … the engine refuses is loud by name … nothing throws" (CK-21; C-E11 §3), and the header's and C-2's "the re-render glide … exact under an ancestor's `zoom` as under its `scale`" (CK-20; C-A11 §3). ## P11-8 · Pass-12 plan

The checklist stays **CK-01..CK-27, unchanged**. Critics may not add rows. Rulings in force: O-103d (1)-(4); O-103f; Q-1 (c) and Q-4 (b) as defaults; Q7 on its default. A ruling that arrives mid-pass is folded in at the next seat boundary, never inside a running battery. Each seat re-reads `../../audit/INBOUND.md` and `../../FORMATION-PROGRESS.md` at its start. The candidate is the in-place construction; there is no second construction and no probe.

**What pass 12 can be.** It is clean only if every held cell reaches D5's base before its critics run: F-17 (D2 converged, which its running pass 11 may give) and F-16 (D3's hunk, not published). On the present record F-16 will not land in time, so pass 12's job is **the fixed point**: CK-20 and CK-21 closed on both critics as classes, each family driven whole (§P11-7 item 8), so that only CK-01, CK-17, CK-19 and CK-25's ambient clause stay open, each held by D2 or D3 (or fewer, if F-17 lands and closes CK-01's latch and CK-17). If pass 12 reaches it, **D5 runs no further build pass until the held cells land**; its next pass is then the first confirmation pass on the base that carries them.

No floor seat unless one of three things happens before P-E12 starts: the base moves (F5⁗ = F5‴ rebased as `§P6-3` lays out, its battery on HEAD's scenes in both engines and F-14's browser cell; the 10.1.x lines rebase with it); D3 publishes the lean lane (F-16 carries the hunk, and P-A12 runs); D2 converges and publishes F-17 (its dock adoption, or a dock scene with its leave and its presses naming their listener). **The third is now live:** D2's confirmation pass 11 is running, and if it closes clean before P-E12 starts, wave 0 records it and F-17 runs at wave 1b. Otherwise F5‴ carries unchanged. If the base moves mid-pass, it is folded in at the next seat boundary.

### P11-8.3 · Seats and order

Every seat is Opus, declared explicitly. D5 runs at most three seats at a time, counted against every live workflow. Browser runs follow the 2026-10-16 note: no `--light`, one browser per battery with a context per cell, `guarded --exclusive webkit` as the one WebKit lock. Each seat takes its WebKit stages after its Chromium phases, never skipped, with re-read stages queued inside the acquisition and one waiter per chain; **a battery that cannot fit one 90-minute acquisition is split in its plan, before it starts, into named acquisitions** (§P11-7 item 7). Films take no mid-film screenshot. Runners and queues are replaced by rename, never edited in flight. The orchestrator commits a seat's records after the seat returns. Drivers print every patch hash from `shasum`.

### P11-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4` .. `§P10-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 11 is **0**, counted for the one candidate A+E, in place. Convergence comes at the end of pass 13 at the earliest, and needs:
- P-E12's CK-20 and CK-21 classes closed on both critics;
- D2's `dock` adoption and the dock's presses, or a dock scene with D2's leave and its presses naming their listener, in D5's base (W-0.8 §10, F-17);
- D3's amplitude and the lean's own lane in D5's base (through master or F-16), with A's reduce write on them (W-0.1 addendum);
- passes 12 and 13 both clean.

On the present record pass 12 cannot be clean (F-16 is not published), so the realistic earliest is two confirmation passes after the held cells land. F-17 follows D2's convergence, possibly at the close of its running pass 11; F-16 follows D3's hunk or W-0.1's 10.2.0 landing, which follows D2's convergence (O-88). Each pass of delay moves that date by one pass. The count cannot start until the held cells land.

Closed on the candidate: **CK-02..16, 18, 23, 24, 26, 27.** Open: **CK-01, 17, 19, 25** (◇, held by D2 and D3; CK-25 by D3's ambient clause alone) and **CK-20, 21, 22** (D5 can build).

**Pass 12 is not a clean pass. The clean-pass count stays 0. The loop has not reached its fixed point:** CK-20 and CK-21 each hold counterexamples D5 can remove, each read in the engines named below with a cause confirmed in the bytes at this seat; CK-22 reopens on claims those counterexamples falsify and on one claim of a cell that drives nothing. Convergence moves to the end of pass 14 at the earliest by the count, and on the present record to two confirmation passes after the held cells land (§P12-8.4).

The score ran 52 % (pass 4), 37 %, 67 %, 78 %, 78 %, 81 %, 78 %, 78 %, 74 %. **Every item §P11-8.1 sent P-E12 to prove holds on both critics' replays, in both engines, to the decimal**: H12's 39 travel cells, 26 hostglide cells, 6 reach cells, P-E12's 22 origin cells, every pass-11 path. The pass-11 counterexamples are gone. What each critic found lies past H12's enumeration, as the charter told them to drive. Eight findings shape pass 13.

1. **CK-21 breaks in WebKit on the one read P-E12 introduced, and on the library's own default tokens.**
   - `tokenOf` asks Typed OM (`CSSNumericValue.parse(…).to(unit)`). Playwright WebKit 26.5 refuses every math function there, while CSS takes each as one `<time>` (C-A12 `facts-webkit.log`, library-free; Chromium takes all of them).
   - So `--spring-panel-duration`, which the library writes as `calc(var(--spring-panel-settle) * var(--motion-tempo))` (`scheme-spring.css:111`), is refused on every committing control: **WebKit cand12 9 of 9 E-2 scenes log `--spring-panel-duration is unreadable on the host; the engagement it drives paints nothing`**, while the commit paints (E-2 164/0). cand11 0 of 9 WebKit; cand12 and cand11 0 of 9 Chromium (C-A12 §0.1). **This is P-E12's regression, WebKit only**: an always-loud channel, which H12's own E-11 rule scores FAIL.
   - A settle written as `calc()`, `min()`, `max()` or `clamp()` is refused by name while CSS runs it 210 ms: WebKit 12 of 72 NOT TAKEN on all three paths (C-A12 `e11v`), 9 of 57 (C-E12 `ce12-e11`, H12's rule and recorder). Chromium 67/72 (the 5 stated non-counterexamples, §P12-2) and 57/57.
   - `--scale-engage: calc(1 + 0.06)` is refused in WebKit and the held slider paints **inline ×0.0000** (C-E12 §0.4, REPORT): the reach's registered 0 is the paint of a refused input (§P12-7 item 6).
2. **CK-20 breaks on three move families past H12's list, in both engines, on both critics where both drove them.**
   - **A transform the place model does not carry.** `rotate: 10deg`, a 40 px block outside or nested: **6.97 px** on the act's frame (both critics, both engines; 2·40·sin 5°). `skewX(15deg)` outside: 10.44 px (C-E12). Perspective: 1.20-3.92 px (C-A12), 2.22 px (C-E12). **A percent `translate`: 20.00 px**, and **inside the library's own Dialog** (`DialogContent`, `translate: -50% -50%`), a 40 px block inserted above the slider: **24.00 px**, the held slider thrown 24 px *up* before it travels 48 px down; a paragraph removed: 16.55-16.59 px (C-E12 §0.1, both engines; stills §P12-5). Cause in the bytes: `ownScale` keeps only axis lengths, and `travelled` carries each scaled box's origin and nothing a box's angle or size does to its paint.
   - **A re-render the stage does not hear.** `v-show` (style), a class, `hidden`, a text node's data, each moving the host 40 px: **40.00 px in one frame, no glide**, both engines, both critics. Cause in the bytes: `watchWorld` observes `childList` only. C-A12's `allkinds` control reads 8/8 per engine (the measured cause, not the cure's shape: the stage's own inline writes would reach the observer).
   - **A layout move the engine already kept still.** With `overflow-anchor` at its default, a block inserted above the viewport: both engines anchor, the held root does not move in paint, and **the stage glides the layout move anyway**, throwing the root 40.00 px off on the act's frame (C-A12 §0.2, both engines). HEAD paints nothing there: this instance is the stage's own doing. Every H12 hostglide cell sets `overflow-anchor: none`.
   - None of these is P-E12's regression: cand11 fails each (both critics).
3. **CK-22 reopens** (§P12-4). Both critics read the same three falsehoods on these bytes: C-2's consumer line "glides it from where it was painted, under any card transform"; C-9's and `settle.ts`'s "a `calc()` of times is a time" (WebKit); and P-E12 §2.2's origin cell "rotate c", which is `rotate: 0deg; scale: 0.8` (`E12/origin-cells.mjs:15`), the identity cited as a driven rotation (C-A12 §0.3). §P11-4 ruled that the row reopens if a restated claim reads false, and §P11-8.1 asked that each family claim name the cell that drives each member.
4. **What holds, both critics, both engines.** E-10 128/0/0 Chromium, 123/0/0 WebKit; E-11 50/0/5 and 54/0/1; E-13 36/0; CAP 18/0; the zoom family (0.5..2.5, track, `<html>`, zoom with scale); the reverse, release-mid, re-render across the line, press at the line, re-press, wheel; the Dialog lens ×1.600 pinned 0.0 px; the anc kinds; the stacking frame; the list easings refused by name with 0 uncaught; `210ms` on CSS's 210.0 ms on the travel, the host glide and the release carry; `animating` glides nothing. C-E12 adds controls that pass on cand12 and fail on cand11: `matrix(0.8,…) · outside`, `remove · outside · scale 0.8`, `zoom 0.5 + scale 0.8 · nested`, and a CSS-transition scale and a WAAPI translate of the card running across an unrelated mutation (0 glides, 0.00 px).
5. **O-103f's conditions hold, read by non-builders in the same run as the bar.** (1) Idle: Chromium `cand8` 119.8 + 119.8 against `cand12` 119.8 + 119.8 rAF + RO per s (C-A12), 119.8 against 119.9 / 120.0 (C-E12); WebKit 59.9 against 59.9 (both); 0 layouts, 0 recalcs. CPU per frame, C-E11's cell: cand12 at cand11's cost, about 0.02-0.09 ms over cand8, the within-build spread as wide as the gap (C-A12 §4, C-E12 §0.3). (3) The loop stops at release; `plant-loop` red 2/2 per engine. (4) Real Safari UNMEASURED.
6. **One WebKit cell is load-sensitive and unresolved.** `ce11 exit-side` failed 3 of 3 cand12 reads in WebKit at loads 8.9-14.0 (C-E12 §2 item 8: `none` 76.4 and 27.4 px, then 11.0 PASS; `zoom125` 26.6 and 20.7 against 18.6), against a cand11 control that passed once at 15-16. `ca11 exit-side · zoom125` failed once (104.7 px, load 11.1; 23.0 px in C-E12's read) and passed on two re-reads at 5.3 and 6.0 (C-A12 §2). **PLAUSIBLE, cause unproven**; it is not scored as a separate counterexample (CK-20 is open regardless) and goes to H13 (§P12-7 item 8). One candidate cause from the bytes, unproven: `onMove` now runs `writeGeometry`'s two token reads and a computed-style read on every scroll event (C-E12 §5 item 6).
7. **The witness had the same gap a fourth time: a family driven on a subset.** H12 enumerated transforms from the list it was given (none, translate px, scale, zoom, zoom with scale), mutations of one kind (`childList`), anchoring switched off, and value forms without math functions; and no cell read the console on default tokens outside the `--engage-*` census. P-E12's battery inherited each gap, and its one step past H12 (the "rotate" origin cell) drove no rotation. §P11-7 item 8 recurs one level out, as C-E12 §6 says.
8. **This is the third pass in a row whose critics found new members of the same two rows' families** (pass 10: zoom; pass 11: scale outside the box, the list grammar, the unit; pass 12: angle, percent, mutation kind, anchoring, the math grammar in one engine). The charter's rule for a stalled pass is a fresh formulation. The pass-13 plan therefore changes the method, not the rows (§P12-8): P-E13 derives the complete input set before it builds (what can move a held host's painted place; what grammar each token's consumer accepts, per engine), cures by construction against that derivation, and the critics attack the derivation as well as the bytes.

## P12-4 · Where the critics differ, and the ruling

Both critics scored the same bytes (`028c1807…`, src tree `61f7b80ac552`) on the harness's own app under harness `d28f9797…` in both engines. The rule is the plan's: a row they disagree on is OPEN unless one critic's measurement refutes the other's on the same path; a counterexample one critic reproduced stands whichever critic read it.

|---|---|---|---|

The merits agree with the rule. §P11-4 set the trigger for this pass in advance, and §P11-8.1 asked P-E12 to restate each family claim naming its cell. The consumer line's "under any card transform" claims a family wider than the clause above it, which holds on its enumerated members (C-E12 §3). That is §P11-7 item 6's class, placed at CK-22, and it is CK-22's own instance, not a second count of a CK-20 defect. C-A12's "rotate c" finding is a claim of a driven cell that drives nothing, which no other row carries. The two `calc()` sentences are counted under CK-21 and stated here only as what P-E13 restates |

## P12-8 · Pass-13 plan

The checklist stays **CK-01..CK-27, unchanged**. Critics may not add rows. Rulings in force: O-103d (1)-(4); O-103f; Q-1 (c) and Q-4 (b) as defaults; Q7 ruled (O-118 (3)); O-87. A ruling that arrives mid-pass is folded in at the next seat boundary, never inside a running battery. Each seat re-reads `../../audit/INBOUND.md` and `../../FORMATION-PROGRESS.md` at its start. The candidate is the in-place construction; there is no second construction and no probe.

**What pass 13 can be.** It cannot be clean: F-17 follows D2's convergence, the close of D2's pass 13 at the earliest, and F-16 is not published. Its job is **the fixed point**, as pass 12's was: CK-20, CK-21 and CK-22 closed on both critics, so that only CK-01, CK-17, CK-19 and CK-25's ambient clause stay open, each held by D2 or D3. **The method changes because three passes found new members of the same families** (§P12-0 finding 8; the charter's fresh-formulation rule). The E seat derives each family's complete member set first, from the engines' grammar, and cures that set by construction. H13 builds cells from the same derivation, and the critics attack the derivation as well as the bytes. If pass 13 reaches the fixed point, **D5 runs no further build pass until the held cells land**; its next pass is then the first confirmation pass on the base that carries them.

No floor seat unless one of three things happens before P-E13 starts: the base moves (F5⁗ = F5‴ rebased as `§P6-3` lays out, with the 10.1.x lines now through 10.1.4, its battery on HEAD's scenes in both engines and F-14's browser cell); D3 publishes the lean lane (F-16 carries the hunk, and P-A13 runs); D2 converges and publishes F-17. On the present record none will: D2's pass 12 is a build pass. Otherwise F5‴ carries unchanged. If the base moves mid-pass, it is folded in at the next seat boundary.

### P12-8.3 · Seats and order

Every seat is Opus, declared explicitly. D5 runs at most three seats at a time, counted against every live workflow (D2's pass 12 and the 10.1.4 close are live at this seat). Browser runs follow the 2026-10-16 note: no `--light`, one browser per battery with a context per cell, `guarded --exclusive webkit` as the one WebKit lock. Each seat takes its WebKit stages after its Chromium phases, never skipped; a battery that cannot fit one 90-minute acquisition is split in its plan, before it starts, into named acquisitions. ### P12-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4` .. `§P11-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. The clean-pass count after pass 12 is **0**, counted for the one candidate A+E, in place. Convergence comes at the end of pass 14 at the earliest, and needs:
- P-E13's CK-20, CK-21 and CK-22 closed on both critics;
- D2's `dock` adoption and the dock's presses, or a dock scene with D2's leave and its presses naming their listener, in D5's base (W-0.8 §10, F-17);
- D3's amplitude and the lean's own lane in D5's base (through master or F-16), with A's reduce write on them (W-0.1 addendum);
- passes 13 and 14 both clean.

On the present record pass 13 cannot be clean (F-17 waits on D2's convergence, the close of its pass 13 at the earliest; F-16 is not published), so the realistic earliest is two confirmation passes after the held cells land. If pass 13 reaches the fixed point, D5 then waits for F-16 and F-17 (or their 10.2.0 landings through master, which follow D2's convergence, O-88) and runs no build pass until they land. Each pass of delay moves that date by one pass. The count cannot start until the held cells land.

The clean-pass count stays 0.** No row is closed or reopened by a measurement on `cand13`; a critic that reads nothing scores nothing (`§P12-8.3`: "a critic whose WebKit cells are not admitted returns those rows open"). **The loop has not reached its fixed point, and on the static record P-E13 cannot reach it.** Independent of the hold, both critics find members the derivations omit, and several are counterexamples by the bytes or by library-free engine reads in both engines, on CK-20, CK-21 and CK-22 (§P13-2). Two of them are regressions from `cand12` (C-E13 D1; C-A13 §2.4). So whatever the held battery reads, pass 14 is a build pass. Six findings shape the next step.

1. **The flag held all four seats** (header). The candidate has never run in a browser. H13's witnesses are of record in Chromium only, for E-10 and E-11. The completion of pass 13 is the next step (§P13-8), after the owner's reboot decision.
2. **What P-E13 got right, statically, both critics agreeing** (C-A13 §0, C-E13 §0.9): Typed OM is gone from `src` (`grep -rn CSSNumericValue src`: 0, C-A13); the token read goes through a registered property of the consumer's type with negative initial values no `ok` takes; `own()` takes the stage's records before and after each write and hands a foreign one back; the observer hears all three record kinds on `<html>`; the glide is painted − now through a map asked of the engine; a refused reach input writes `1` and says so; P-E12 §2.2's "rotate c" is corrected; the speedtest `SubnetAddDialog.vue` site (`<DialogContent>` `:3`, the held control `:19`, `e2d9444d`) and the `--engage-read-` census (0 in every sibling) reproduce (C-A13 §3). On the affine members H13 drives, the construction is exact by arithmetic (C-E13 §0.9). None of this closes a row.
3. **The WebKit engine facts P-E13 rests on mostly hold**, read library-free by C-A13 in WebKit 26.5 (`facts-webkit.log`, load 8.4-12.1): a registered `<time>`, `<number>` or `<length>` takes every math form and serializes it canonically (P-E13 §9 item 2 holds); a just-created paused `composite: "add"` translate applies to `getBoundingClientRect()` synchronously, 40.000 px plain and (39.392, 6.946) under `rotate: 10deg` (§9 item 3 holds); anchoring and the clamp are applied before a `MutationObserver` callback reads (`facts-webkit-anchor.log`). **One fact breaks:** in WebKit a `transition: all` on the element read makes the registered read return its initial `-1s` (C-A13 §2.7; Chromium unaffected). **One fact stays unread in both engines:** the document timeline's time across tasks between frames (C-E13 F4), on which P-E13 (a)(vii) rests.
4. **The counterexamples that stand on the bytes or on engine reads** (§P13-2 lists each with its grade):
   - CK-20: a script-animated ancestor (inline style written per frame, as the library's own `CarouselContent.vue:65-66` does) is glided every frame, a regression on `cand12` (C-E13 D1); the lens's scale read as bounding box over layout box paints the loupe ×9.67 tall under `rotate: 10deg` on a 300×6 track, a regression on `cand12` (C-A13 §2.4); the "Newton step" is a chord step, landing 1.74-133.81 px off under steeper perspective or longer moves, with H13's one perspective member at the one setting under 0.5 px (C-A13 §2.1, both engines; C-E13 D4 by model); an animation the record itself starts reads 60 against 100 in the observer callback, so its own entrance is glided (C-A13 §2.2, both engines); a shadow-tree edit, a `field-sizing: content` textarea's `value`, and a `<link>` stylesheet at load move a later box with no record (C-A13 §2.3, both engines); a page loop requested before the stage compounds the "one frame" bound every frame (C-E13 D2); a released host glides an ancestor's animation, a case P-E13 names and leaves uncured (C-E13 D3).
   - CK-21: the library's consumers substitute `--scale-engage` and `--spring-dock-settle` inside `calc()`, so `1 + 0.06` and `2 * 0.53` compute 1.06 in the consumer and are refused by the read in both engines, and `0.1s + 110ms` is refused in Chromium (C-A13 §2.6); `transition: all` in WebKit (finding 3); `--motion-tempo` at 0 or below runs the travel and the release carry 210 ms with no error while CSS runs them at 0s, because `motionTempo()` maps it to 1 silently (C-E13 D7; this seat read `motionTempo.ts:38-40`).
   - CK-22: `stage.ts:50-52` and C-2's "an ancestor's running animation is never glided" against P-E13's own (a)(vii) and released-host limits; the header's "every transform … carried as they paint" and "one Newton step"; `settle.ts`'s "takes exactly the forms the consumer's type takes, in every engine"; P-E13 §1.2's "including stylesheet elements" (`<link>`); `engage-stage.css:13` citing a deleted `writeGeometry` and `:15-16` saying an unreadable host "reads 0" where the bytes write `1`; C-2's "under every transform between the control and the page" (C-A13 §3, C-E13 §3; this seat read the CSS lines in the patch).
5. **The method held at one level and broke at the next.** §P12-8 changed the method so that the E seat derives each family's member set before it builds. Both seats did, and both critics attacked the derivations as the plan asked. The critics found members past them in four places: the consumer's expression (a `calc()` fragment, not a type), the record grammar (shadow trees, IDL form state, resource loads), the projective part (sampled at one mild setting), and time (unequal at the two reads when the record starts the animation or a script writes the animation). This is §P12-7 item 5 a fifth time, now at the derivation's level (C-A13 §0.5; C-E13 §5 items 1, 3, 4). C-E13's class 1 names the root: **the construction partitions moves by mechanism (a record to glide, time to leave), while the row asks for continuity in paint**, and an animation built from records belongs to both. H13 named the alternative itself: a no-record layout move "a record-driven glide cannot hear … and a frame-driven one can" (H13 §1.1 (i)). The charter's fresh-formulation rule applies to pass 14 (§P13-8.3).

## P13-8 · The next step: the pass-13 completion, then pass 14

The checklist stays **CK-01..CK-27, unchanged**. Critics may not add rows. Rulings in force as in the header. A ruling that arrives mid-pass is folded in at the next seat boundary, never inside a running battery. Each seat re-reads `../../audit/INBOUND.md` and `../../FORMATION-PROGRESS.md` at its start.

### P13-8.1 · The precondition

**No D5 seat starts until `ZONE-ARMED` clears or the owner reboots.** The owner decides; D5 has no lever on it. The completion seats compete for the same admission as D2's pass-12 completion and the 10.1.4 release close, so the orchestrator sums every live run's widest remaining pool against the 5-6 ceiling before launching (at most three D5 seats at a time).

### P13-8.2 · The completion seats and their order

The completion measures `cand13` as built; it changes no candidate byte. A seat that finds a defect reports it; it does not cure it.

If any completion seat is held by the flag again, it returns PARTIAL with its held list, as these four did, and the next completion runs it unchanged.

### P13-8.3 · What pass 14 must be, on this record

Pass 14 is a **build pass**: P-E14 on P-E13 (or on P-E12, if the completion reads a `cand13` regression on rows `cand12` closed that P-E14 cannot cure in place; c4 rules the base). Three passes found new members of the same two families by enumeration, and pass 13's derivation found them at the derivation's level (§P13-0 finding 5). The charter's rule for a stalled pass is a fresh formulation, so c4's plan starts from §P13-7 item 3: **the row asks for continuity in paint, and a cure that partitions moves by mechanism will meet another member.** c4 sets the plan; it names at least that P-E14's derivation states each engine fact with its read in each engine, enumerates token reads by call graph and each consumer by its expression, and states a parameter range per member that bounds the family, and that H14 builds its cells from the same statement. A held control that is not glided at all under some member is admissible only as a stated limit the critics can test, never as a claim the header widens.

### P13-8.4 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4` .. `§P12-8.4`): at least three full passes (met at pass 3); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. **The clean-pass count after pass 13 is 0**, counted for the one candidate A+E, in place. Pass 13 is not clean, and pass 14 is a build pass with CK-20, CK-21 and CK-22 to close, so convergence comes at the end of **pass 15 at the earliest** by the count, and needs:
- P-E14's CK-20, CK-21 and CK-22 closed on both critics;
- D2's `dock` adoption and the dock's presses, or a dock scene with D2's leave and its presses naming their listener, in D5's base (W-0.8 §10, F-17), which waits on D2's convergence (its pass 13's close at the earliest);
- D3's amplitude and the lean's own lane in D5's base (through master or F-16), with A's reduce write on them (W-0.1 addendum);
- passes 14 and 15 both clean.

On the present record the realistic earliest is two confirmation passes after the held cells land. **The fixed point is not reached, so D5 does not yet wait on D2 and D3: it completes pass 13 and builds pass 14.** When a pass reaches the fixed point, D5 then waits for F-16 and F-17 (or their 10.2.0 landings through master, which follow D2's convergence, O-88) and runs no build pass until they land. **The R-K1-c vehicle moves with this registry again:** it was "after pass 14 at the earliest"; it is now after pass 15 at the earliest. atlas's M-7 stays until then.

## P13c-6 · The base of P-E14: **P-E13**

§P13-8.3's rule: P-E14 builds on P-E12 only if the completion reads a cand13 regression, on rows cand12 closed, that P-E14 cannot cure in place. Two rows regress: CK-04 and CK-09.
- **CK-09** is cured in place by moving three registered names out of `--engage-*`.
- **CK-04** is cured in place by reading the reach's frame again on whatever invalidates it, as cand12 did per scroll.
- **CK-20's regressions** (finding 2) are inside a row that is open on both builds. They come from the one mechanism the fresh formulation replaces (finding 3), so going back to P-E12 would not cure them.

Going back would also discard what cand13 alone carries: rotation, skew, percent `translate`, the Dialog, every attribute and text kind, anchoring, the WebKit default-token census, and the lower cost. **P-E14 builds on P-E13, in place, one arm.** Its derivation cites cand12 as the passing reference on each member set cand12 carried (class 2). A's part stays P-A7's bytes unless D3's lane arrives.

Each seat re-reads `../../audit/INBOUND.md` and `../../FORMATION-PROGRESS.md` at its start. Every seat is Opus, declared explicitly.

The orchestrator sums every live run's widest remaining pool against the 5-6 ceiling, runs at most three D5 seats at a time, and holds one WebKit lock machine-wide.

### P13c-8.1 · What pass 14 must be: a build pass under a fresh formulation

Three passes found new members of the same families by enumeration, and pass 13 found them at the derivation's level. The completion measured why: the construction sorts moves by mechanism (a record to glide, time to leave), and an animation made of records, a record that starts an animation, a page loop writing every frame and a move with no record each fall on the wrong side (finding 3). The charter's rule for a stalled pass is a fresh formulation (CHARTER line 78). **The row asks for continuity in paint. Pass 14 starts from that statement, not from a list of mechanisms.**

**The formulation P-E14 derives from (S14, a section of `P-E14.md` written and challenged before any byte is built):**

- **(a) The invariant, stated on paint.** Read the held host's painted place once per frame in the frame of the box that carries it. Between two frames it never steps by more than the row's bound (≤ 0.5 px on the act's frame; ≤ H14's bound over any 16.7 ms). A move of the carrier (any ancestor's transform, animation, scroll or script-written style, at any rate) is carried and never resisted. A move of the host's place inside its carrier that the engine paints as a step is glided from where it was painted. The stage's own glide is one more move under the same invariant, so it must land.
- **(b) The decomposition S14 must choose and defend.** S14 separates "carried" from "stepped" by the geometry the engine paints, never by which observer heard it. H13 §1.1 (i) named a frame-driven read that hears a move with no record. A split of the painted map into the carrier's map and the host's offset inside it is the other construction in view. S14 picks one, or a third, and shows that every member below falls on the side the invariant asks for. **A layout-property animation** (a script or transition writing `top`, `margin` or `width` of an ancestor every frame) belongs to both families. S14 states its treatment and the member's range.
- **(c) Each member with a parameter range that bounds its family,** and the reason the range bounds it (the extremal cell, or a monotone error in the parameter). The minimum sets, from this pass's records:
  - perspective: P 200-600 px, θ 0-60°, insert 40-200 px, host −200 to +150 px from the origin, inside and outside the tilted card (CA13c §2.1, CE13c D4, pass 12's setups);
  - rotation, skew, scale and their compositions on the lens, over track aspects 300 × 6 to 140 × 76 (CA13c §2.4);
  - script-animated ancestors: inline `translate`, `scale`, a custom property (the Carousel's `--member-scale`), a scroll-linked parallax; amplitude 0-80 px, period 200-600 ms; page loops requested before and after the stage's (CE13c D1, D2);
  - animations a record starts, on C and on G (CA13c §2.2), and CSS transitions under a page loop in WebKit (CE13c D2);
  - moves with no record: shadow trees, `field-sizing` IDL state, `<link>`, font and image loads, CSSOM edits, pseudo-class restyles (CA13c §2.3);
  - the host's own size change, left-aligned and centred, with the grow's share stated (CA13c §2.5, CE13c D5);
  - a card scaled about the held control during the hold, 0.8-1.25 (CE13c D9);
  - the released host on its exit clock under an ancestor's animation (CE13c D3);
  - the clamp in a scroller and on the page, in both engines (E13c §2.1);
  - the exit-side cells with cand11 as the passing reference (H13c §5);
  - and **every member cand12 passes** (class 2).
  A held control that is not glided at all under some member is admissible **only as a stated limit** with its read in each engine, for the critics to test, never as a claim a header widens.
- **(d) Every engine fact S14 uses, with its read in each engine.** Cite H13c §1 (F1g, F2, F3, F4 with WebKit's 1 ms, F6, F7) or read a new fact library-free under `guarded` on a fresh element with the consumer elsewhere (class 6 (g)).
- **(e) The token reads by call graph, each consumer by its expression.** Enumerate from `tokenOf`, `easingOf`, `rowClockMs` and `motionTempo()` by call, not by name. For each token, the library's own consumer expression is read in the bytes (`engage.css`'s `calc(1 + (var(--scale-engage) - 1) * …)`, `scheme-spring.css`'s settles, the gutter's page padding), and the read takes exactly what that expression computes, in that consumer's context (`em`, `cq*`; D8). The read sits where the page's cascade cannot narrow it (F7), or the limit is stated with its read in each engine. It leaves no DOM residue (class 3). Its private names sit outside `--engage-*` (class 1). `--motion-tempo` ≤ 0 is either the clock CSS runs or one loud error, never a silent 1 (E-2).
- **(f) CK-04's frame** is read again on whatever invalidates it (D9).
- **(g) CK-22:** every header sentence and every comment is read against S14's own limits, with no comment left on deleted code. A MIGRATION row covers every consumer-visible effect (residue, names on `:root`, forced work).
- **(h) Cost:** the drag's forced layouts and recalcs priced beside cand12, plus CPU per frame held still beside cand8, cand11, cand12 and cand13.

### P13c-8.3 · Convergence criterion

Unchanged (`§3.5`, `§P1-9.4`, `§P2-8.4` .. `§P13-8.4`): at least three full passes (met); zero open rows against CK-01..CK-27; a fresh adversarial audit by seats that did not author the winning spec; two consecutive clean passes. **The clean-pass count after pass 13 is 0.**

Pass 14 cannot be clean. CK-01 and CK-17 need F-17 in D5's base, and F-17 follows D2's convergence, which comes at D2's pass 14 close at the earliest. F-16 is not published. So **D5's convergence comes at the close of pass 16 at the earliest** on the present record (§P13-8.4 said pass 15). That needs all of:
- P-E14's CK-04, 09, 20, 21 and 22 and CK-25's lens clause closed on both critics;
- F-17 and F-16 (or their 10.2.0 landings through master) in D5's base, with A's reduce write on D3's lane;
- passes 15 and 16 both clean.

If pass 14 reaches the fixed point, D5 runs no further build pass until F-16 and F-17 land. **The R-K1-c vehicle moves with this registry: after pass 16 at the earliest.** atlas's M-7 stays until then.
